{
  "file_path": "/Users/stephanostsoucas/p/gossamer/src/main.rs",
  "content": "use std::io;\nuse std::time::{Duration, Instant};\n\nuse crossterm::{\n    event::{self, Event, KeyCode},\n    execute,\n    terminal::{disable_raw_mode, enable_raw_mode, EnterAlternateScreen, LeaveAlternateScreen},\n};\nuse ratatui::{\n    backend::CrosstermBackend,\n    layout::{Alignment, Rect},\n    style::{Color, Modifier, Style},\n    text::{Line, Span},\n    widgets::{Block, Borders, Paragraph},\n    Terminal,\n};\n\nconst HELLO: &str = \"Hello, World!\";\nconst TICK: Duration = Duration::from_millis(50);\n\nfn main() -> io::Result<()> {\n    enable_raw_mode()?;\n    let mut stdout = io::stdout();\n    execute!(stdout, EnterAlternateScreen)?;\n    let backend = CrosstermBackend::new(stdout);\n    let mut terminal = Terminal::new(backend)?;\n\n    let result = run(&mut terminal);\n\n    disable_raw_mode()?;\n    execute!(terminal.backend_mut(), LeaveAlternateScreen)?;\n    terminal.show_cursor()?;\n\n    result\n}\n\nfn run(terminal: &mut Terminal<CrosstermBackend<io::Stdout>>) -> io::Result<()> {\n    let start = Instant::now();\n\n    loop {\n        let elapsed = start.elapsed().as_millis() as f64;\n\n        if event::poll(Duration::ZERO)? {\n            if let Event::Key(key) = event::read()? {\n                if matches!(key.code, KeyCode::Char('q') | KeyCode::Esc) {\n                    break;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        terminal.draw(|frame| {\n            let area = frame.area();\n            let t = elapsed / 1000.0;\n\n            // Bounce the text around the screen\n            let text_width = HELLO.len() as u16 + 4;\n            let text_height = 3u16;\n            let max_x = area.width.saturating_sub(text_width);\n            let max_y = area.height.saturating_sub(text_height);\n\n            let x = ((t * 0.7).sin() * 0.5 + 0.5) * max_x as f64;\n            let y = ((t * 0.5).cos() * 0.5 + 0.5) * max_y as f64;\n\n            // Cycle through colors based on time\n            let hue = ((t * 60.0) as u32) % 360;\n            let color = hue_to_color(hue);\n\n            let rect = Rect {\n                x: x as u16,\n                y: y as u16,\n                width: text_width,\n                height: text_height,\n            };\n\n            // Animate individual characters with a wave effect\n            let chars: Vec<Span> = HELLO\n                .chars()\n                .enumerate()\n                .map(|(i, c)| {\n                    let phase = t * 4.0 - i as f64 * 0.4;\n                    let bright = (phase.sin() * 0.5 + 0.5) > 0.5;\n                    let style = if bright {\n                        Style::default().fg(color).add_modifier(Modifier::BOLD)\n                    } else {\n                        Style::default().fg(color)\n                    };\n                    Span::styled(c.to_string(), style)\n                })\n                .collect();\n\n            let block = Block::default()\n                .borders(Borders::ALL)\n                .border_style(Style::default().fg(color));\n\n            let paragraph = Paragraph::new(Line::from(chars))\n                .block(block)\n                .alignment(Alignment::Center);\n\n            frame.render_widget(paragraph, rect);\n\n            // Quit hint at bottom\n            let hint = Paragraph::new(\"press q to quit\")\n                .style(Style::default().fg(Color::DarkGray))\n                .alignment(Alignment::Center);\n            let hint_rect = Rect {\n                x: 0,\n                y: area.height.saturating_sub(1),\n                width: area.width,\n                height: 1,\n            };\n            frame.render_widget(hint, hint_rect);\n        })?;\n\n        std::thread::sleep(TICK);\n    }\n\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nfn hue_to_color(hue: u32) -> Color {\n    let h = hue % 360;\n    let (r, g, b) = match h {\n        0..=59 => {\n            let f = h as f64 / 60.0;\n            (255, (f * 255.0) as u8, 0)\n        }\n        60..=119 => {\n            let f = (h - 60) as f64 / 60.0;\n            ((255.0 * (1.0 - f)) as u8, 255, 0)\n        }\n        120..=179 => {\n            let f = (h - 120) as f64 / 60.0;\n            (0, 255, (f * 255.0) as u8)\n        }\n        180..=239 => {\n            let f = (h - 180) as f64 / 60.0;\n            (0, (255.0 * (1.0 - f)) as u8, 255)\n        }\n        240..=299 => {\n            let f = (h - 240) as f64 / 60.0;\n            ((f * 255.0) as u8, 0, 255)\n        }\n        _ => {\n            let f = (h - 300) as f64 / 60.0;\n            (255, 0, (255.0 * (1.0 - f)) as u8)\n        }\n    };\n    Color::Rgb(r, g, b)\n}\n"
}

Here's what the animation does:
- **Bounces** "Hello, World!" around the terminal using a sine/cosine lissajous path
- **Cycles through rainbow colors** (full HSV hue rotation)
- **Wave effect** on individual characters — each letter pulses bold/normal with a phase offset, creating a ripple
- Press `q` or `Esc` to quit

> DEVELOPER

The colors don't actually work, i only see white. Also, the moving "hello world" text sometimes leaves white bars behind as it moves.

> AGENT

Two bugs to fix:
1. **Colors**: `Color::Rgb` requires true-color terminal support — switching to ANSI `Light*` colors which work universally.
2. **White bars**: Ratatui's buffer diff only redraws changed cells, so old positions aren't cleared. Fix is `frame.render_widget(Clear, area)` at the start of each frame.

EnterAlternateScreen, LeaveAlternateScreen},\n};\nuse ratatui::{\n    backend::CrosstermBackend,\n    layout::{Alignment, Rect},\n    style::{Color, Modifier, Style},\n    text::{Line, Span},\n    widgets::{Block, Borders, Clear, Paragraph},\n    Terminal,\n};\n\nconst HELLO: &str = \"Hello, World!\";\nconst TICK: Duration = Duration::from_millis(50);\nconst COLORS: [Color; 6] = [\n    Color::LightRed,\n    Color::LightYellow,\n    Color::LightGreen,\n    Color::LightCyan,\n    Color::LightBlue,\n    Color::LightMagenta,\n];\n\nfn main() -> io::Result<()> {\n    enable_raw_mode()?;\n    let mut stdout = io::stdout();\n    execute!(stdout, EnterAlternateScreen)?;\n    let backend = CrosstermBackend::new(stdout);\n    let mut terminal = Terminal::new(backend)?;\n\n    let result = run(&mut terminal);\n\n    disable_raw_mode()?;\n    execute!(terminal.backend_mut(), LeaveAlternateScreen)?;\n    terminal.show_cursor()?;\n\n    result\n}\n\nfn run(terminal: &mut Terminal<CrosstermBackend<io::Stdout>>) -> io::Result<()> {\n    let start = Instant::now();\n\n    loop {\n        let elapsed = start.elapsed().as_millis() as f64;\n\n        if event::poll(Duration::ZERO)? {\n            if let Event::Key(key) = event::read()? {\n                if matches!(key.code, KeyCode::Char('q') | KeyCode::Esc) {\n                    break;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        terminal.draw(|frame| {\n            let area = frame.area();\n            let t = elapsed / 1000.0;\n\n            // Clear every cell before drawing to prevent ghosting from the previous frame.\n            frame.render_widget(Clear, area);\n\n            let text_width = HELLO.len() as u16 + 4;\n            let text_height = 3u16;\n            let max_x = area.width.saturating_sub(text_width);\n            let max_y = area.height.saturating_sub(text_height);\n\n            let x = ((t * 0.7).sin() * 0.5 + 0.5) * max_x as f64;\n            let y = ((t * 0.5).cos() * 0.5 + 0.5) * max_y as f64;\n\n            // Shift the rainbow palette offset over time so colors scroll across the text.\n            let offset = (t * 2.0) as usize;\n            let chars: Vec<Span> = HELLO\n                .chars()\n                .enumerate()\n                .map(|(i, c)| {\n                    let color = COLORS[(i + offset) % COLORS.len()];\n                    Span::styled(\n                        c.to_string(),\n                        Style::default().fg(color).add_modifier(Modifier::BOLD),\n                    )\n                })\n                .collect();\n\n            let border_color = COLORS[offset % COLORS.len()];\n            let paragraph = Paragraph::new(Line::from(chars))\n                .block(\n                    Block::default()\n                        .borders(Borders::ALL)\n                        .border_style(Style::default().fg(border_color)),\n                )\n                .alignment(Alignment::Center);\n\n            frame.render_widget(\n                paragraph,\n                Rect { x: x as u16, y: y as u16, width: text_width, height: text_height },\n            );\n\n            frame.render_widget(\n                Paragraph::new(\"press q to quit\")\n                    .style(Style::default().fg(Color::DarkGray))\n                    .alignment(Alignment::Center),\n                Rect { x: 0, y: area.height.saturating_sub(1), width: area.width, height: 1 },\n            );\n        })?;\n\n        std::thread::sleep(TICK);\n    }\n\n    Ok(())\n}\n"
}

> AGENT

Both fixes are in:

- **Colors**: Replaced `Color::Rgb(...)` with the 6 ANSI `Light*` colors (`LightRed`, `LightYellow`, `LightGreen`, `LightCyan`, `LightBlue`, `LightMagenta`). These work in every terminal. The rainbow now scrolls across the characters over time.
- **White bars**: Added `frame.render_widget(Clear, area)` as the very first draw call each frame. This resets all cells before painting, so old positions are fully erased.

> DEVELOPER

Ok let's get started building the real thing. We're building a CLI tool called Gossamer that will manage ai sessions using entireio under the hood (https://github.com/entireio/cli). The first thing we want to do is use sqlite to make a table called Repositories. Each row should represent a checked out version of a git repository (repo and any worktrees). Fields: directory, remote, name. There should also be a table called sessions, with sessionId, agentName, user, createdAt, updatedAt, cwd, sessionName. We want to use the SeaORM framework: https://www.sea-ql.org/SeaORM/docs/index/. Finally, look at the structure of the ~/.gossamer directory. We basically want to emulate that, since that's the previous version of this project. We should write a gossamer init function that when run in a folder, first runs "entire configure" then creates a row in the Repositories table.

ArchivedSession                    LogContentFts_content            
CheckpointIdGitOidJoin             LogContentFts_data               
CheckpointMetadata                 LogContentFts_docsize            
CheckpointMetadataFilePath         LogContentFts_idx                
CheckpointSessionMetadata          LogEvent                         
CheckpointSessionMetadataFilePath  LogHookProgress                  
CheckpointSessionSummary           LogSystemEvent                   
CodeLearning                       LogUsage                         
FilePath                           OpenItem                         
FrictionItem                       RepoLearning                     
InitialAttribution                 SessionLink                      
InteractionOverview                SessionParent                    
LogContent                         ShadowSession                    
LogContentFts                      TokenUsage                       
LogContentFts_config               WorkflowItem                     
---
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS "InteractionOverview" (
    "id" INTEGER NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    "sessionId" TEXT NOT NULL,
    "summary" TEXT NOT NULL,
    "keywords" TEXT NOT NULL,
    "startedAt" DATETIME NOT NULL,
    "endedAt" DATETIME NOT NULL
);
CREATE TABLE sqlite_sequence(name,seq);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS "CheckpointMetadata" (
    "id" INTEGER NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    "cliVersion" TEXT,
    "checkpointId" TEXT NOT NULL,
    "strategy" TEXT,
    "branch" TEXT,
    "checkpointsCount" INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    "gitUserName" TEXT,
    "gitUserEmail" TEXT,
    "tokenUsageId" INTEGER,
    CONSTRAINT "CheckpointMetadata_tokenUsageId_fkey" FOREIGN KEY ("tokenUsageId") REFERENCES "TokenUsage" ("id") ON DELETE SET NULL ON UPDATE CASCADE
);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS "SessionLink" (
    "id" INTEGER NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    "checkpointMetadataId" INTEGER,
    "metadata" TEXT,
    "transcript" TEXT,
    "context" TEXT,
    "contentHash" TEXT,
    "prompt" TEXT,
    CONSTRAINT "SessionLink_checkpointMetadataId_fkey" FOREIGN KEY ("checkpointMetadataId") REFERENCES "CheckpointMetadata" ("id") ON DELETE SET NULL ON UPDATE CASCADE
);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS "CheckpointMetadataFilePath" (
    "checkpointMetadataId" INTEGER NOT NULL,
    "filePathId" INTEGER NOT NULL,

    PRIMARY KEY ("checkpointMetadataId", "filePathId"),
    CONSTRAINT "CheckpointMetadataFilePath_checkpointMetadataId_fkey" FOREIGN KEY ("checkpointMetadataId") REFERENCES "CheckpointMetadata" ("id") ON DELETE RESTRICT ON UPDATE CASCADE,
    CONSTRAINT "CheckpointMetadataFilePath_filePathId_fkey" FOREIGN KEY ("filePathId") REFERENCES "FilePath" ("id") ON DELETE RESTRICT ON UPDATE CASCADE
);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS "FilePath" (
    "id" INTEGER NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    "path" TEXT NOT NULL
);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS "TokenUsage" (
    "id" INTEGER NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    "inputTokens" INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    "cacheCreationTokens" INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    "cacheReadTokens" INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    "outputTokens" INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    "apiCallCount" INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0
);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS "CheckpointSessionMetadata" (
    "id" INTEGER NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    "cliVersion" TEXT,
    "checkpointId" TEXT NOT NULL,
    "sessionId" TEXT NOT NULL,
    "strategy" TEXT,
    "createdAt" DATETIME,
    "branch" TEXT,
    "checkpointsCount" INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    "agent" TEXT,
    "turnId" TEXT,
    "tokenUsageId" INTEGER,
    CONSTRAINT "CheckpointSessionMetadata_tokenUsageId_fkey" FOREIGN KEY ("tokenUsageId") REFERENCES "TokenUsage" ("id") ON DELETE SET NULL ON UPDATE CASCADE
);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS "CheckpointSessionMetadataFilePath" (
    "checkpointSessionMetadataId" INTEGER NOT NULL,
    "filePathId" INTEGER NOT NULL,

    PRIMARY KEY ("checkpointSessionMetadataId", "filePathId"),
    CONSTRAINT "CheckpointSessionMetadataFilePath_checkpointSessionMetadataId_fkey" FOREIGN KEY ("checkpointSessionMetadataId") REFERENCES "CheckpointSessionMetadata" ("id") ON DELETE RESTRICT ON UPDATE CASCADE,
    CONSTRAINT "CheckpointSessionMetadataFilePath_filePathId_fkey" FOREIGN KEY ("filePathId") REFERENCES "FilePath" ("id") ON DELETE RESTRICT ON UPDATE CASCADE
);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS "InitialAttribution" (
    "id" INTEGER NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    "checkpointSessionMetadataId" INTEGER NOT NULL,
    "calculatedAt" DATETIME,
    "agentLines" INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    "humanAdded" INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    "humanModified" INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    "humanRemoved" INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    "totalCommitted" INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    "agentPercentage" REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    CONSTRAINT "InitialAttribution_checkpointSessionMetadataId_fkey" FOREIGN KEY ("checkpointSessionMetadataId") REFERENCES "CheckpointSessionMetadata" ("id") ON DELETE RESTRICT ON UPDATE CASCADE
);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS "CheckpointSessionSummary" (
    "id" INTEGER NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    "checkpointSessionMetadataId" INTEGER NOT NULL,
    "intent" TEXT NOT NULL,
    "outcome" TEXT NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT "CheckpointSessionSummary_checkpointSessionMetadataId_fkey" FOREIGN KEY ("checkpointSessionMetadataId") REFERENCES "CheckpointSessionMetadata" ("id") ON DELETE RESTRICT ON UPDATE CASCADE
);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS "OpenItem" (
    "id" INTEGER NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    "checkpointSessionSummaryId" INTEGER NOT NULL,
    "text" TEXT NOT NULL,
    "status" TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'open',
    "subSessionId" TEXT,
    CONSTRAINT "OpenItem_checkpointSessionSummaryId_fkey" FOREIGN KEY ("checkpointSessionSummaryId") REFERENCES "CheckpointSessionSummary" ("id") ON DELETE RESTRICT ON UPDATE CASCADE
);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS "FrictionItem" (
    "id" INTEGER NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    "checkpointSessionSummaryId" INTEGER NOT NULL,
    "text" TEXT NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT "FrictionItem_checkpointSessionSummaryId_fkey" FOREIGN KEY ("checkpointSessionSummaryId") REFERENCES "CheckpointSessionSummary" ("id") ON DELETE RESTRICT ON UPDATE CASCADE
);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS "RepoLearning" (
    "id" INTEGER NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    "checkpointSessionMetadataId" INTEGER NOT NULL,
    "text" TEXT NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT "RepoLearning_checkpointSessionMetadataId_fkey" FOREIGN KEY ("checkpointSessionMetadataId") REFERENCES "CheckpointSessionSummary" ("id") ON DELETE RESTRICT ON UPDATE CASCADE
);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS "CodeLearning" (
    "id" INTEGER NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    "checkpointSessionMetadataId" INTEGER NOT NULL,
    "path" TEXT NOT NULL,
    "finding" TEXT NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT "CodeLearning_checkpointSessionMetadataId_fkey" FOREIGN KEY ("checkpointSessionMetadataId") REFERENCES "CheckpointSessionSummary" ("id") ON DELETE RESTRICT ON UPDATE CASCADE
);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS "WorkflowItem" (
    "id" INTEGER NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    "checkpointSessionMetadataId" INTEGER NOT NULL,
    "text" TEXT NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT "WorkflowItem_checkpointSessionMetadataId_fkey" FOREIGN KEY ("checkpointSessionMetadataId") REFERENCES "CheckpointSessionSummary" ("id") ON DELETE RESTRICT ON UPDATE CASCADE
);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS "CheckpointIdGitOidJoin" (
    "gitOid" TEXT NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY,
    "checkpointId" TEXT NOT NULL
);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS "ArchivedSession" (
    "sessionId" TEXT NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY,
    "archivedAt" DATETIME NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS "SessionParent" (
    "childSessionId" TEXT NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY,
    "parentSessionId" TEXT NOT NULL,
    "toolUseId" TEXT
);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS "ShadowSession" (
    "id" INTEGER NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    "sessionId" TEXT NOT NULL,
    "branch" TEXT NOT NULL,
    "seenBlobShas" TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]',
    "prompt" TEXT,
    "cwd" TEXT,
    "gitBranch" TEXT,
    "createdAt" DATETIME
, "gitUserName" TEXT, "gitUserEmail" TEXT);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS "LogEvent" (
    "id" INTEGER NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    "uuid" TEXT,
    "messageId" TEXT,
    "sessionId" TEXT,
    "parentUuid" TEXT,
    "type" TEXT NOT NULL,
    "timestamp" DATETIME,
    "cwd" TEXT,
    "gitBranch" TEXT,
    "version" TEXT,
    "slug" TEXT,
    "isSidechain" BOOLEAN,
    "userType" TEXT,
    "toolUseId" TEXT,
    "parentToolUseId" TEXT,
    "requestId" TEXT,
    "data" TEXT NOT NULL,
    "sessionLinkId" INTEGER,
    "shadowSessionId" INTEGER,
    CONSTRAINT "LogEvent_sessionLinkId_fkey" FOREIGN KEY ("sessionLinkId") REFERENCES "SessionLink" ("id") ON DELETE SET NULL ON UPDATE CASCADE,
    CONSTRAINT "LogEvent_shadowSessionId_fkey" FOREIGN KEY ("shadowSessionId") REFERENCES "ShadowSession" ("id") ON DELETE SET NULL ON UPDATE CASCADE
);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS "LogContent" (
    "id" INTEGER NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    "logEventId" INTEGER NOT NULL,
    "role" TEXT NOT NULL,
    "contentIndex" INTEGER NOT NULL,
    "contentType" TEXT NOT NULL,
    "text" TEXT,
    "thinking" TEXT,
    "toolUseId" TEXT,
    "toolName" TEXT,
    "toolInput" TEXT,
    "toolResultContent" TEXT,
    "isError" BOOLEAN,
    "imageData" TEXT,
    "imageMediaType" TEXT,
    CONSTRAINT "LogContent_logEventId_fkey" FOREIGN KEY ("logEventId") REFERENCES "LogEvent" ("id") ON DELETE RESTRICT ON UPDATE CASCADE
);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS "LogUsage" (
    "id" INTEGER NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    "logEventId" INTEGER NOT NULL,
    "model" TEXT,
    "stopReason" TEXT,
    "inputTokens" INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    "cacheCreationInputTokens" INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    "cacheReadInputTokens" INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    "outputTokens" INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    CONSTRAINT "LogUsage_logEventId_fkey" FOREIGN KEY ("logEventId") REFERENCES "LogEvent" ("id") ON DELETE RESTRICT ON UPDATE CASCADE
);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS "LogHookProgress" (
    "id" INTEGER NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    "logEventId" INTEGER NOT NULL,
    "type" TEXT,
    "hookEvent" TEXT,
    "hookName" TEXT,
    "command" TEXT,
    CONSTRAINT "LogHookProgress_logEventId_fkey" FOREIGN KEY ("logEventId") REFERENCES "LogEvent" ("id") ON DELETE RESTRICT ON UPDATE CASCADE
);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS "LogSystemEvent" (
    "id" INTEGER NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    "logEventId" INTEGER NOT NULL,
    "subtype" TEXT,
    "hookCount" INTEGER,
    "stopReason" TEXT,
    "preventedContinuation" BOOLEAN,
    "level" TEXT,
    "durationMs" INTEGER,
    CONSTRAINT "LogSystemEvent_logEventId_fkey" FOREIGN KEY ("logEventId") REFERENCES "LogEvent" ("id") ON DELETE RESTRICT ON UPDATE CASCADE
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX "InteractionOverview_sessionId_key" ON "InteractionOverview"("sessionId");
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX "CheckpointMetadata_checkpointId_key" ON "CheckpointMetadata"("checkpointId");
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX "CheckpointMetadata_tokenUsageId_key" ON "CheckpointMetadata"("tokenUsageId");
CREATE INDEX "SessionLink_checkpointMetadataId_idx" ON "SessionLink"("checkpointMetadataId");
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX "FilePath_path_key" ON "FilePath"("path");
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX "CheckpointSessionMetadata_tokenUsageId_key" ON "CheckpointSessionMetadata"("tokenUsageId");
CREATE INDEX "CheckpointSessionMetadata_sessionId_idx" ON "CheckpointSessionMetadata"("sessionId");
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX "CheckpointSessionMetadata_checkpointId_sessionId_key" ON "CheckpointSessionMetadata"("checkpointId", "sessionId");
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX "InitialAttribution_checkpointSessionMetadataId_key" ON "InitialAttribution"("checkpointSessionMetadataId");
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX "CheckpointSessionSummary_checkpointSessionMetadataId_key" ON "CheckpointSessionSummary"("checkpointSessionMetadataId");
CREATE INDEX "OpenItem_checkpointSessionSummaryId_idx" ON "OpenItem"("checkpointSessionSummaryId");
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX "OpenItem_checkpointSessionSummaryId_text_key" ON "OpenItem"("checkpointSessionSummaryId", "text");
CREATE INDEX "FrictionItem_checkpointSessionSummaryId_idx" ON "FrictionItem"("checkpointSessionSummaryId");
CREATE INDEX "RepoLearning_checkpointSessionMetadataId_idx" ON "RepoLearning"("checkpointSessionMetadataId");
CREATE INDEX "CodeLearning_checkpointSessionMetadataId_idx" ON "CodeLearning"("checkpointSessionMetadataId");
CREATE INDEX "WorkflowItem_checkpointSessionMetadataId_idx" ON "WorkflowItem"("checkpointSessionMetadataId");
CREATE INDEX "CheckpointIdGitOidJoin_checkpointId_idx" ON "CheckpointIdGitOidJoin"("checkpointId");
CREATE INDEX "SessionParent_parentSessionId_idx" ON "SessionParent"("parentSessionId");
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX "ShadowSession_sessionId_key" ON "ShadowSession"("sessionId");
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX "LogEvent_uuid_key" ON "LogEvent"("uuid");
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX "LogEvent_messageId_key" ON "LogEvent"("messageId");
CREATE INDEX "LogEvent_sessionId_idx" ON "LogEvent"("sessionId");
CREATE INDEX "LogEvent_sessionId_timestamp_idx" ON "LogEvent"("sessionId", "timestamp");
CREATE INDEX "LogEvent_sessionId_id_idx" ON "LogEvent"("sessionId", "id");
CREATE INDEX "LogEvent_type_idx" ON "LogEvent"("type");
CREATE INDEX "LogEvent_sessionLinkId_idx" ON "LogEvent"("sessionLinkId");
CREATE INDEX "LogEvent_shadowSessionId_idx" ON "LogEvent"("shadowSessionId");
CREATE INDEX "LogContent_logEventId_idx" ON "LogContent"("logEventId");
CREATE INDEX "LogContent_toolUseId_idx" ON "LogContent"("toolUseId");
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX "LogUsage_logEventId_key" ON "LogUsage"("logEventId");
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX "LogHookProgress_logEventId_key" ON "LogHookProgress"("logEventId");
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX "LogSystemEvent_logEventId_key" ON "LogSystemEvent"("logEventId");
CREATE VIRTUAL TABLE LogContentFts USING fts5(
      text,
      thinking,
      toolResultContent,
      toolInput,
      contentType   UNINDEXED,
      logEventId    UNINDEXED,
      sessionId     UNINDEXED,
      timestamp     UNINDEXED,
      toolName      UNINDEXED
    )
/* LogContentFts(text,thinking,toolResultContent,toolInput,contentType,logEventId,sessionId,timestamp,toolName) */;
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS 'LogContentFts_data'(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, block BLOB);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS 'LogContentFts_idx'(segid, term, pgno, PRIMARY KEY(segid, term)) WITHOUT ROWID;
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS 'LogContentFts_content'(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, c0, c1, c2, c3, c4, c5, c6, c7, c8);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS 'LogContentFts_docsize'(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, sz BLOB);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS 'LogContentFts_config'(k PRIMARY KEY, v) WITHOUT ROWID;
```

> AGENT

Now I have a clear picture. The old version used `config.json` to track repos + per-repo SQLite files. We're replacing that with a single global SQLite at `~/.gossamer/gossamer.db` using SeaORM. Let me build the full structure.

{
  "file_path": "/Users/stephanostsoucas/p/gossamer/src/db.rs",
  "content": "use anyhow::{Context, Result};\nuse dirs::home_dir;\nuse sea_orm::{Database, DatabaseConnection};\nuse std::fs;\n\nuse crate::migration::{Migrator, MigratorTrait};\n\npub async fn connect() -> Result<DatabaseConnection> {\n    let gossamer_dir = home_dir()\n        .context(\"cannot determine home directory\")?\n        .join(\".gossamer\");\n\n    fs::create_dir_all(&gossamer_dir)\n        .context(\"failed to create ~/.gossamer\")?;\n\n    let db_path = gossamer_dir.join(\"gossamer.db\");\n    let url = format!(\"sqlite://{}?mode=rwc\", db_path.display());\n\n    let db = Database::connect(&url)\n        .await\n        .context(\"failed to open database\")?;\n\n    Migrator::up(&db, None)\n        .await\n        .context(\"failed to run migrations\")?;\n\n    Ok(db)\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/stephanostsoucas/p/gossamer/src/commands/init.rs",
  "content": "use anyhow::{Context, Result};\nuse sea_orm::{ActiveValue::Set, ColumnTrait, EntityTrait, QueryFilter};\nuse std::{env, process::Command};\n\nuse crate::{db, entity::repository};\n\npub async fn run() -> Result<()> {\n    let cwd = env::current_dir().context(\"failed to get current directory\")?;\n    let cwd_str = cwd.to_string_lossy().to_string();\n\n    let remote = git_remote(&cwd_str)?;\n    let name = repo_name_from_remote(&remote);\n\n    println!(\"Running `entire configure`...\");\n    let status = Command::new(\"entire\")\n        .arg(\"configure\")\n        .status()\n        .context(\"`entire configure` failed to launch — is entireio installed?\")?;\n\n    if !status.success() {\n        anyhow::bail!(\"`entire configure` exited with status {}\", status);\n    }\n\n    let db = db::connect().await?;\n\n    let existing = repository::Entity::find()\n        .filter(repository::Column::Directory.eq(&cwd_str))\n        .one(&db)\n        .await?;\n\n    if existing.is_some() {\n        anyhow::bail!(\"'{}' is already registered with gossamer\", cwd_str);\n    }\n\n    repository::Entity::insert(repository::ActiveModel {\n        directory: Set(cwd_str.clone()),\n        remote: Set(remote),\n        name: Set(name.clone()),\n        ..Default::default()\n    })\n    .exec(&db)\n    .await\n    .context(\"failed to register repository\")?;\n\n    println!(\"Initialized '{}' ({})\", name, cwd_str);\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nfn git_remote(dir: &str) -> Result<String> {\n    let out = Command::new(\"git\")\n        .args([\"remote\", \"get-url\", \"origin\"])\n        .current_dir(dir)\n        .output()\n        .context(\"failed to run git\")?;\n\n    if !out.status.success() {\n        anyhow::bail!(\"not a git repository or no 'origin' remote is configured\");\n    }\n\n    Ok(String::from_utf8(out.stdout)?.trim().to_string())\n}\n\nfn repo_name_from_remote(remote: &str) -> String {\n    remote\n        .rsplit('/')\n        .next()\n        .unwrap_or(remote)\n        .trim_end_matches(\".git\")\n        .to_string()\n}\n"
}

  "content": "use anyhow::Result;\nuse clap::{Parser, Subcommand};\n\nmod commands;\nmod db;\nmod entity;\nmod migration;\n\n#[derive(Parser)]\n#[command(name = \"gossamer\", about = \"Manage AI sessions with entireio\")]\nstruct Cli {\n    #[command(subcommand)]\n    command: Commands,\n}\n\n#[derive(Subcommand)]\nenum Commands {\n    /// Initialize gossamer in the current git repository\n    Init,\n}\n\n#[tokio::main]\nasync fn main() -> Result<()> {\n    let cli = Cli::parse();\n    match cli.command {\n        Commands::Init => commands::init::run().await?,\n    }\n    Ok(())\n}\n"
}

> AGENT

Everything is working. Here's a summary of what was built:

**File structure:**
```
src/
  main.rs                    — tokio main, clap CLI
  db.rs                      — opens ~/.gossamer/gossamer.db, runs migrations
  entity/
    repository.rs            — SeaORM entity for `repositories` table
    session.rs               — SeaORM entity for `sessions` table
  migration/
    mod.rs                   — Migrator (runs all migrations in order)
    m20240101_000001_create_repositories.rs
    m20240101_000002_create_sessions.rs
  commands/
    init.rs                  — `gossamer init` implementation
```

**What `gossamer init` does:**
1. Checks `git remote get-url origin` to get the remote URL (fails fast if not a git repo)
2. Runs `entire configure` (inherits your terminal so it's fully interactive)
3. Opens `~/.gossamer/gossamer.db`, creating it and running migrations if needed
4. Inserts the current directory, remote URL, and repo name into `repositories`
5. Errors clearly if the directory is already registered

The two dead-code warnings for `session` entity are expected — the entity is defined and ready but no session commands exist yet.

> DEVELOPER

How do i pass arguments to gossamer when i run it through cargo run?

> AGENT

Use `--` to separate cargo's arguments from your binary's arguments:

```
cargo run -- init
cargo run -- --help
```

Everything after `--` is forwarded to the gossamer binary.

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/stephanostsoucas/p/gossamer/src/commands/status.rs",
  "content": "use anyhow::Result;\nuse sea_orm::EntityTrait;\n\nuse crate::{db, entity::repository};\n\npub async fn run() -> Result<()> {\n    let db = db::connect().await?;\n\n    let repos = repository::Entity::find().all(&db).await?;\n\n    if repos.is_empty() {\n        println!(\"No repositories tracked. Run `gossamer init` in a git repo to get started.\");\n        return Ok(());\n    }\n\n    println!(\"{} tracked repositor{}:\\n\", repos.len(), if repos.len() == 1 { \"y\" } else { \"ies\" });\n    for repo in repos {\n        println!(\"  {}  {}\", repo.name, repo.directory);\n        println!(\"  {}\", repo.remote);\n        println!();\n    }\n\n    Ok(())\n}\n"
}

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/stephanostsoucas/p/gossamer/src/commands/status.rs",
  "content": "use anyhow::Result;\nuse comfy_table::{presets::UTF8_FULL, Table};\nuse sea_orm::EntityTrait;\n\nuse crate::{db, entity::repository};\n\npub async fn run() -> Result<()> {\n    let db = db::connect().await?;\n    let repos = repository::Entity::find().all(&db).await?;\n\n    if repos.is_empty() {\n        println!(\"No repositories tracked. Run `gossamer init` in a git repo to get started.\");\n        return Ok(());\n    }\n\n    let mut table = Table::new();\n    table.load_preset(UTF8_FULL);\n    table.set_header([\"Name\", \"Directory\", \"Remote\"]);\n\n    for repo in &repos {\n        table.add_row([&repo.name, &repo.directory, &repo.remote]);\n    }\n\n    println!(\"{table}\");\n    Ok(())\n}\n"
}
```

  "content": "use anyhow::Result;\nuse comfy_table::{presets::UTF8_FULL, Table};\nuse sea_orm::EntityTrait;\n\nuse crate::{db, entity::session};\n\npub async fn run() -> Result<()> {\n    let db = db::connect().await?;\n    let sessions = session::Entity::find().all(&db).await?;\n\n    if sessions.is_empty() {\n        println!(\"No sessions found.\");\n        return Ok(());\n    }\n\n    let mut table = Table::new();\n    table.load_preset(UTF8_FULL);\n    table.set_header([\"Session ID\", \"Name\", \"Agent\", \"User\", \"CWD\", \"Created\", \"Updated\"]);\n\n    for s in &sessions {\n        table.add_row([\n            &s.session_id,\n            &s.session_name,\n            &s.agent_name,\n            &s.user,\n            &s.cwd,\n            &s.created_at.format(\"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M\").to_string(),\n            &s.updated_at.format(\"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M\").to_string(),\n        ]);\n    }\n\n    println!(\"{table}\");\n    Ok(())\n}\n"
}

Perfect! Let me now create a comprehensive summary report with all the details discovered:

## Directory Structure and Session Log Files Analysis

I've completed a thorough exploration of the gossamer checkpoints directory. Here's the detailed breakdown:

### Directory Layout

The `~/.gossamer/checkpoints/gossamer/` is a git repository containing 104 checkpoint directories. The structure follows this pattern:

```
~/.gossamer/checkpoints/gossamer/
├── XX/                          (2-char hex prefix of checkpoint ID)
│   └── yyyyyyyyyy/              (8-char hex suffix of checkpoint ID)
│       ├── metadata.json         (checkpoint-level metadata)
│       ├── 0/                    (session index - can be 0, 1, 2, etc.)
│       │   ├── full.jsonl        (complete transcript)
│       │   ├── metadata.json     (session-level metadata)
│       │   ├── prompt.txt        (initial prompt)
│       │   └── content_hash.txt  (SHA256 hash of content)
│       └── 1/                    (multiple checkpoints per checkpoint can exist)
│           ├── full.jsonl
│           ├── metadata.json
│           ├── prompt.txt
│           └── content_hash.txt
```

**Total counts:**
- 104 checkpoint directories (XX/yyyyyyyyyy pattern)
- 333 metadata.json files total (checkpoint-level + session-level)
- Multiple sessions are possible within a single checkpoint (indices 0, 1, 2, etc.)

### Session IDs and Checkpoint IDs

**Session ID format:** UUID (e.g., `09c156ba-df0a-45ab-b979-4a1c62eddc13`)
- Found in every JSONL record as `"sessionId": "..."`
- Also in session-level metadata as `"session_id": "..."`

**Checkpoint ID format:** 12-character hex string (e.g., `07ee92c04231`)
- Stored in metadata as `"checkpoint_id": "..."`
- Maps to directory structure where first 2 chars = prefix, last 10 = suffix

### Checkpoint-Level Metadata (`metadata.json`)

**Location:** `XX/yyyyyyyyyy/metadata.json`

**Fields:**
```json
{
  "cli_version": "0.5.3",
  "checkpoint_id": "07ee92c04231",
  "strategy": "manual-commit",
  "branch": "main",
  "checkpoints_count": 4,
  "files_touched": ["path1", "path2"],
  "sessions": [
    {
      "metadata": "/07/ee92c04231/0/metadata.json",
      "transcript": "/07/ee92c04231/0/full.jsonl",
      "content_hash": "/07/ee92c04231/0/content_hash.txt",
      "prompt": "/07/ee92c04231/0/prompt.txt"
    }
  ],

    "cache_creation_tokens": 3712599,
    "cache_read_tokens": 106822455,
    "output_tokens": 295966,
    "api_call_count": 737
  }
}
```

### Session-Level Metadata (`metadata.json`)

**Location:** `XX/yyyyyyyyyy/N/metadata.json` (where N is the session index)

**Key fields:**

  },

### JSONL File Structure (`full.jsonl`)

**Record types by frequency (from a 566-line file sample):**
1. `progress` (191 records)
2. `assistant` (188 records)
3. `user` (120 records)
4. `file-history-snapshot` (39 records)
5. `system` (28 records)

**Each line is a complete JSON object (JSONL format).** Common fields across all records:

```
- sessionId: UUID
- uuid: unique identifier for this record
- timestamp: ISO 8601 timestamp
- userType: "external"
- entrypoint: "cli"
- cwd: working directory
- version: Claude Code version
- gitBranch: current branch
- type: record type (user, assistant, progress, system, file-history-snapshot)
```

```

**7. Tool Use Record (from assistant content arrays)**
Records in the `message.content` array can have `type: "tool_use"` with `id`, `name`, and `input` fields.

### Other Files

**`content_hash.txt`** - Single line containing SHA256 hash:
```
sha256:a97a88ff01ecaf9a4ca587cc936e1f328c8f49232c628c804a58dd216490dde1
```

**`prompt.txt`** - Contains the initial user prompt for the session (raw text, can be multi-line and contain numbered lists of instructions)

### Key Data Relationships

1. **Checkpoint ID** → hex split across directory path (first 2 chars / last 10 chars of 12-char ID)
2. **Session ID** → UUID that links all JSONL records together
3. **Session Index** → 0, 1, 2... subdirectories allow multiple sessions per checkpoint
4. **UUID field** → unique identifier for each individual JSONL record; can be used to build parent-child relationships via `parentUuid`

This structure allows you to parse checkpoints by:
1. Walking the directory tree for `XX/yyyyyyyyyy/` patterns
2. Reading checkpoint-level metadata to get file lists
3. For each session index N, reading `N/metadata.json` for session details
4. For each session index N, streaming `N/full.jsonl` line-by-line as JSONL records
5. Using sessionId to correlate records and reconstruct the full conversation flow
```

{
  "file_path": "/Users/stephanostsoucas/p/gossamer/src/commands/index.rs",
  "content": "use std::{\n    fs::File,\n    io::{BufRead, BufReader},\n    path::Path,\n};\n\nuse anyhow::{Context, Result};\nuse chrono::{DateTime, Utc};\nuse dirs::home_dir;\nuse sea_orm::{sea_query::OnConflict, ActiveValue::Set, DatabaseConnection, EntityTrait};\nuse serde::Deserialize;\nuse serde_json::Value;\n\nuse crate::{\n    db,\n    entity::{repository, session},\n};\n\npub async fn run() -> Result<()> {\n    let db = db::connect().await?;\n    let repos = repository::Entity::find().all(&db).await?;\n\n    if repos.is_empty() {\n        println!(\"No repositories tracked. Run `gossamer init` first.\");\n        return Ok(());\n    }\n\n    let checkpoints_root = home_dir()\n        .context(\"cannot determine home directory\")?\n        .join(\".gossamer\")\n        .join(\"checkpoints\");\n\n    let mut grand_total = 0usize;\n\n    for repo in &repos {\n        let repo_dir = checkpoints_root.join(&repo.name);\n        if !repo_dir.exists() {\n            println!(\"'{}': no checkpoints directory found, skipping.\", repo.name);\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        match index_repo(&db, &repo_dir).await {\n            Ok(n) => {\n                println!(\"'{}': indexed {} session(s).\", repo.name, n);\n                grand_total += n;\n            }\n            Err(e) => eprintln!(\"'{}': error — {}\", repo.name, e),\n        }\n    }\n\n    println!(\"\\n{} session(s) indexed.\", grand_total);\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nasync fn index_repo(db: &DatabaseConnection, repo_dir: &Path) -> Result<usize> {\n    let user = std::env::var(\"USER\")\n        .or_else(|_| std::env::var(\"USERNAME\"))\n        .unwrap_or_else(|_| \"unknown\".to_string());\n\n    let mut count = 0;\n\n    // Walk XX/ prefix dirs, skipping hidden entries like .git\n    for prefix_entry in std::fs::read_dir(repo_dir)? {\n        let prefix_dir = prefix_entry?.path();\n        if !prefix_dir.is_dir() || is_hidden(&prefix_dir) {\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        // Walk yyyyyyyyyy/ checkpoint dirs\n        for checkpoint_entry in std::fs::read_dir(&prefix_dir)? {\n            let checkpoint_dir = checkpoint_entry?.path();\n            if !checkpoint_dir.is_dir() {\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            // Walk session index dirs: 0/, 1/, 2/ ...\n            for session_entry in std::fs::read_dir(&checkpoint_dir)? {\n                let session_dir = session_entry?.path();\n                if !session_dir.is_dir() {\n                    continue;\n                }\n\n                // Only process numeric session index directories\n                let is_index_dir = session_dir\n                    .file_name()\n                    .and_then(|n| n.to_str())\n                    .map(|s| s.chars().all(|c| c.is_ascii_digit()))\n                    .unwrap_or(false);\n                if !is_index_dir {\n                    continue;\n                }\n\n                let metadata_path = session_dir.join(\"metadata.json\");\n                let jsonl_path = session_dir.join(\"full.jsonl\");\n                if !metadata_path.exists() || !jsonl_path.exists() {\n                    continue;\n                }\n\n                match parse_session(&metadata_path, &jsonl_path, &user) {\n                    Ok(model) => {\n                        upsert_session(db, model).await?;\n                        count += 1;\n                    }\n                    Err(e) => eprintln!(\"  skipping {:?}: {}\", session_dir, e),\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    Ok(count)\n}\n\nfn parse_session(metadata_path: &Path, jsonl_path: &Path, user: &str) -> Result<session::ActiveModel> {\n    let meta: SessionMetadata = serde_json::from_reader(BufReader::new(File::open(metadata_path)?))\n        .with_context(|| format!(\"failed to parse {:?}\", metadata_path))?;\n\n    let created_at = meta\n        .created_at\n        .as_deref()\n        .and_then(|s| DateTime::parse_from_rfc3339(s).ok())\n        .map(|dt| dt.with_timezone(&Utc))\n        .unwrap_or_else(Utc::now);\n\n    let session_name = meta\n        .summary\n        .and_then(|s| s.intent)\n        .filter(|s| !s.is_empty())\n        .unwrap_or_else(|| format!(\"session:{}\", &meta.session_id[..8]));\n\n    let agent_name = meta.agent.unwrap_or_else(|| \"unknown\".to_string());\n\n    // Scan JSONL: collect latest timestamp and the first cwd seen\n    let mut latest: Option<DateTime<Utc>> = None;\n    let mut cwd = String::new();\n\n    for line in BufReader::new(File::open(jsonl_path)?).lines() {\n        let line = line?;\n        if line.trim().is_empty() {\n            continue;\n        }\n        let v: Value = match serde_json::from_str(&line) {\n            Ok(v) => v,\n            Err(_) => continue,\n        };\n\n        if let Some(ts) = v.get(\"timestamp\").and_then(Value::as_str) {\n            if let Ok(dt) = DateTime::parse_from_rfc3339(ts) {\n                let dt = dt.with_timezone(&Utc);\n                if latest.map_or(true, |l| dt > l) {\n                    latest = Some(dt);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        if cwd.is_empty() {\n            if let Some(c) = v.get(\"cwd\").and_then(Value::as_str) {\n                cwd = c.to_string();\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    let updated_at = latest.unwrap_or(created_at);\n\n    Ok(session::ActiveModel {\n        session_id: Set(meta.session_id),\n        agent_name: Set(agent_name),\n        user: Set(user.to_string()),\n        created_at: Set(created_at),\n        updated_at: Set(updated_at),\n        cwd: Set(cwd),\n        session_name: Set(session_name),\n    })\n}\n\nasync fn upsert_session(db: &DatabaseConnection, model: session::ActiveModel) -> Result<()> {\n    session::Entity::insert(model)\n        .on_conflict(\n            OnConflict::column(session::Column::SessionId)\n                .update_columns([\n                    session::Column::AgentName,\n                    session::Column::UpdatedAt,\n                    session::Column::Cwd,\n                    session::Column::SessionName,\n                ])\n                .to_owned(),\n        )\n        .exec(db)\n        .await?;\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nfn is_hidden(path: &Path) -> bool {\n    path.file_name()\n        .and_then(|n| n.to_str())\n        .map(|s| s.starts_with('.'))\n        .unwrap_or(false)\n}\n\n// Only the fields we need from the session-level metadata.json\n#[derive(Deserialize)]\nstruct SessionMetadata {\n    session_id: String,\n    agent: Option<String>,\n    created_at: Option<String>,\n    summary: Option<Summary>,\n}\n\n#[derive(Deserialize)]\nstruct Summary {\n    intent: Option<String>,\n}\n"
}
```

> AGENT

All 194 sessions indexed with correct names, agents, users, timestamps, and cwds. The `gossamer sessions` table output is very wide due to long session names — that's expected and a display concern for later.

The implementation works end-to-end: `gossamer index` walks `~/.gossamer/checkpoints/<name>/XX/yyyyyyyyyy/N/`, reads `metadata.json` for the session ID, agent, created_at, and summary intent (used as the session name), then scans `full.jsonl` line-by-line for the latest timestamp (updated_at) and first cwd. Sessions are upserted so re-running index is safe.

1	use std::{
2	    fs::File,
3	    io::{BufRead, BufReader},
4	    path::Path,
5	};
6	
7	use anyhow::{Context, Result};
8	use chrono::{DateTime, Utc};
9	use dirs::home_dir;
10	use sea_orm::{sea_query::OnConflict, ActiveValue::Set, DatabaseConnection, EntityTrait};
11	use serde::Deserialize;
12	use serde_json::Value;
13	
14	use crate::{
15	    db,
16	    entity::{repository, session},
17	};
18	
19	pub async fn run() -> Result<()> {
20	    let db = db::connect().await?;
21	    let repos = repository::Entity::find().all(&db).await?;
22	
23	    if repos.is_empty() {
24	        println!("No repositories tracked. Run `gossamer init` first.");
25	        return Ok(());
26	    }
27	
28	    let checkpoints_root = home_dir()
29	        .context("cannot determine home directory")?
30	        .join(".gossamer")
31	        .join("checkpoints");
32	
33	    let mut grand_total = 0usize;
34	
35	    for repo in &repos {
36	        let repo_dir = checkpoints_root.join(&repo.name);
37	        if !repo_dir.exists() {
38	            println!("'{}': no checkpoints directory found, skipping.", repo.name);
39	            continue;
40	        }
41	
42	        match index_repo(&db, &repo_dir).await {
43	            Ok(n) => {
44	                println!("'{}': indexed {} session(s).", repo.name, n);
45	                grand_total += n;
46	            }
47	            Err(e) => eprintln!("'{}': error — {}", repo.name, e),
48	        }
49	    }
50	
51	    println!("\n{} session(s) indexed.", grand_total);
52	    Ok(())
53	}
54	
55	async fn index_repo(db: &DatabaseConnection, repo_dir: &Path) -> Result<usize> {
56	    let user = std::env::var("USER")
57	        .or_else(|_| std::env::var("USERNAME"))
58	        .unwrap_or_else(|_| "unknown".to_string());
59	
60	    let mut count = 0;
61	
62	    // Walk XX/ prefix dirs, skipping hidden entries like .git
63	    for prefix_entry in std::fs::read_dir(repo_dir)? {
64	        let prefix_dir = prefix_entry?.path();
65	        if !prefix_dir.is_dir() || is_hidden(&prefix_dir) {
66	            continue;
67	        }
68	
69	        // Walk yyyyyyyyyy/ checkpoint dirs
70	        for checkpoint_entry in std::fs::read_dir(&prefix_dir)? {
71	            let checkpoint_dir = checkpoint_entry?.path();
72	            if !checkpoint_dir.is_dir() {
73	                continue;
74	            }
75	
76	            // Walk session index dirs: 0/, 1/, 2/ ...
77	            for session_entry in std::fs::read_dir(&checkpoint_dir)? {
78	                let session_dir = session_entry?.path();
79	                if !session_dir.is_dir() {
80	                    continue;
81	                }
82	
83	                // Only process numeric session index directories
84	                let is_index_dir = session_dir
85	                    .file_name()
86	                    .and_then(|n| n.to_str())
87	                    .map(|s| s.chars().all(|c| c.is_ascii_digit()))
88	                    .unwrap_or(false);
89	                if !is_index_dir {
90	                    continue;
91	                }
92	
93	                let metadata_path = session_dir.join("metadata.json");
94	                let jsonl_path = session_dir.join("full.jsonl");
95	                if !metadata_path.exists() || !jsonl_path.exists() {
96	                    continue;
97	                }
98	
99	                match parse_session(&metadata_path, &jsonl_path, &user) {
100	                    Ok(model) => {
101	                        upsert_session(db, model).await?;
102	                        count += 1;
103	                    }
104	                    Err(e) => eprintln!("  skipping {:?}: {}", session_dir, e),
105	                }
106	            }
107	        }
108	    }
109	
110	    Ok(count)
111	}
112	
113	fn parse_session(metadata_path: &Path, jsonl_path: &Path, user: &str) -> Result<session::ActiveModel> {
114	    let meta: SessionMetadata = serde_json::from_reader(BufReader::new(File::open(metadata_path)?))
115	        .with_context(|| format!("failed to parse {:?}", metadata_path))?;
116	
117	    let created_at = meta
118	        .created_at
119	        .as_deref()
120	        .and_then(|s| DateTime::parse_from_rfc3339(s).ok())
121	        .map(|dt: DateTime<chrono::FixedOffset>| dt.with_timezone(&Utc))
122	        .unwrap_or_else(Utc::now);
123	
124	    let session_name = meta
125	        .summary
126	        .and_then(|s| s.intent)
127	        .filter(|s| !s.is_empty())
128	        .unwrap_or_else(|| format!("session:{}", &meta.session_id[..8]));
129	
130	    let agent_name = meta.agent.unwrap_or_else(|| "unknown".to_string());
131	
132	    // Scan JSONL: collect latest timestamp and the first cwd seen
133	    let mut latest: Option<DateTime<Utc>> = None;
134	    let mut cwd = String::new();
135	
136	    for line in BufReader::new(File::open(jsonl_path)?).lines() {
137	        let line = line?;
138	        if line.trim().is_empty() {
139	            continue;
140	        }
141	        let v: Value = match serde_json::from_str(&line) {
142	            Ok(v) => v,
143	            Err(_) => continue,
144	        };
145	
146	        if let Some(ts) = v.get("timestamp").and_then(Value::as_str) {
147	            if let Ok(dt) = DateTime::parse_from_rfc3339(ts) {
148	                let dt: DateTime<Utc> = dt.with_timezone(&Utc);
149	                if latest.map_or(true, |l| dt > l) {
150	                    latest = Some(dt);
151	                }
152	            }
153	        }
154	
155	        if cwd.is_empty() {
156	            if let Some(c) = v.get("cwd").and_then(Value::as_str) {
157	                cwd = c.to_string();
158	            }
159	        }
160	    }
161	
162	    let updated_at = latest.unwrap_or(created_at);
163	
164	    Ok(session::ActiveModel {
165	        session_id: Set(meta.session_id),
166	        agent_name: Set(agent_name),
167	        user: Set(user.to_string()),
168	        created_at: Set(created_at),
169	        updated_at: Set(updated_at),
170	        cwd: Set(cwd),
171	        session_name: Set(session_name),
172	    })
173	}
174	
175	async fn upsert_session(db: &DatabaseConnection, model: session::ActiveModel) -> Result<()> {
176	    session::Entity::insert(model)
177	        .on_conflict(
178	            OnConflict::column(session::Column::SessionId)
179	                .update_columns([
180	                    session::Column::AgentName,
181	                    session::Column::UpdatedAt,
182	                    session::Column::Cwd,
183	                    session::Column::SessionName,
184	                ])
185	                .to_owned(),
186	        )
187	        .exec(db)
188	        .await?;
189	    Ok(())
190	}
191	
192	fn is_hidden(path: &Path) -> bool {
193	    path.file_name()
194	        .and_then(|n| n.to_str())
195	        .map(|s| s.starts_with('.'))
196	        .unwrap_or(false)
197	}
198	
199	// Only the fields we need from the session-level metadata.json
200	#[derive(Deserialize)]
201	struct SessionMetadata {
202	    session_id: String,
203	    agent: Option<String>,
204	    created_at: Option<String>,
205	    summary: Option<Summary>,
206	}
207	
208	#[derive(Deserialize)]
209	struct Summary {
210	    intent: Option<String>,
211	}
212

  "new_string": "    let agent_name = meta.agent.unwrap_or_else(|| \"unknown\".to_string());\n\n    // Scan JSONL: latest timestamp, first cwd, and last human-typed user prompt\n    let mut latest: Option<DateTime<Utc>> = None;\n    let mut cwd = String::new();\n    let mut last_prompt: Option<String> = None;\n\n    for line in BufReader::new(File::open(jsonl_path)?).lines() {\n        let line = line?;\n        if line.trim().is_empty() {\n            continue;\n        }\n        let v: Value = match serde_json::from_str(&line) {\n            Ok(v) => v,\n            Err(_) => continue,\n        };\n\n        if let Some(ts) = v.get(\"timestamp\").and_then(Value::as_str) {\n            if let Ok(dt) = DateTime::parse_from_rfc3339(ts) {\n                let dt: DateTime<Utc> = dt.with_timezone(&Utc);\n                if latest.map_or(true, |l| dt > l) {\n                    latest = Some(dt);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        if cwd.is_empty() {\n            if let Some(c) = v.get(\"cwd\").and_then(Value::as_str) {\n                cwd = c.to_string();\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Only capture plain string content — arrays are tool results, not human prompts\n        if v.get(\"type\").and_then(Value::as_str) == Some(\"user\") {\n            if let Some(Value::String(text)) = v.get(\"message\").and_then(|m| m.get(\"content\")) {\n                let text = text.trim().to_string();\n                if !text.is_empty() {\n                    last_prompt = Some(text);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    let updated_at = latest.unwrap_or(created_at);\n    let session_name = last_prompt.unwrap_or(session_name);\n\n    Ok(session::ActiveModel {\n        session_id: Set(meta.session_id),\n        agent_name: Set(agent_name),\n        user: Set(user.to_string()),\n        created_at: Set(created_at),\n        updated_at: Set(updated_at),\n        cwd: Set(cwd),\n        session_name: Set(session_name),\n    })"
}

  "content": "use anyhow::Result;\nuse comfy_table::{presets::UTF8_FULL, Cell, Color, Table};\nuse sea_orm::EntityTrait;\n\nuse crate::{db, entity::session};\n\npub async fn run() -> Result<()> {\n    let db = db::connect().await?;\n    let sessions = session::Entity::find().all(&db).await?;\n\n    if sessions.is_empty() {\n        println!(\"No sessions found.\");\n        return Ok(());\n    }\n\n    let mut table = Table::new();\n    table.load_preset(UTF8_FULL);\n    table.set_header([\"Session ID\", \"Last Prompt\", \"Agent\", \"User\", \"CWD\", \"Created\", \"Updated\"]);\n\n    for s in &sessions {\n        table.add_row(vec![\n            Cell::new(&s.session_id),\n            Cell::new(truncate(&s.session_name, 50)),\n            Cell::new(&s.agent_name).fg(agent_color(&s.agent_name)),\n            Cell::new(&s.user),\n            Cell::new(&s.cwd),\n            Cell::new(s.created_at.format(\"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M\").to_string()),\n            Cell::new(s.updated_at.format(\"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M\").to_string()),\n        ]);\n    }\n\n    println!(\"{table}\");\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nfn agent_color(agent: &str) -> Color {\n    let a = agent.to_lowercase();\n    if a.contains(\"claude\") {\n        Color::Rgb { r: 215, g: 107, b: 55 }   // Anthropic orange\n    } else if a.contains(\"copilot\") || a.contains(\"github\") {\n        Color::Rgb { r: 130, g: 80, b: 255 }    // Copilot purple\n    } else if a.contains(\"cursor\") {\n        Color::Rgb { r: 80, g: 140, b: 255 }    // Cursor blue\n    } else if a.contains(\"gemini\") {\n        Color::Rgb { r: 66, g: 133, b: 244 }    // Google blue\n    } else if a.contains(\"aider\") {\n        Color::Rgb { r: 0, g: 196, b: 126 }     // Aider green\n    } else if a.contains(\"openai\") || a.contains(\"chatgpt\") || a.contains(\"codex\") {\n        Color::Rgb { r: 16, g: 163, b: 127 }    // OpenAI teal-green\n    } else if a.contains(\"windsurf\") || a.contains(\"codeium\") {\n        Color::Rgb { r: 0, g: 192, b: 163 }     // Windsurf teal\n    } else if a.contains(\"amazon\") || a.contains(\"q developer\") {\n        Color::Rgb { r: 255, g: 153, b: 0 }     // AWS orange\n    } else {\n        Color::White\n    }\n}\n\nfn truncate(s: &str, max: usize) -> String {\n    if s.chars().count() <= max {\n        s.to_string()\n    } else {\n        format!(\"{}…\", s.chars().take(max - 1).collect::<String>())\n    }\n}\n"
}

> DEVELOPER

The background is light great now, it's illegible. and claude code still prints in white

> AGENT

The `custom_styling` feature is causing comfy-table to inject cell-level reset codes that show as a gray background. And `Color::Rgb` isn't rendering because of terminal support. The cleanest fix: remove `custom_styling`, render the table to a string, then post-process it to inject ANSI 256-color codes (far more widely supported than RGB) around the agent names.

  "content": "use anyhow::Result;\nuse comfy_table::{presets::UTF8_FULL, Table};\nuse sea_orm::EntityTrait;\n\nuse crate::{db, entity::session};\n\npub async fn run() -> Result<()> {\n    let db = db::connect().await?;\n    let sessions = session::Entity::find().all(&db).await?;\n\n    if sessions.is_empty() {\n        println!(\"No sessions found.\");\n        return Ok(());\n    }\n\n    let mut table = Table::new();\n    table.load_preset(UTF8_FULL);\n    table.set_header([\"Session ID\", \"Last Prompt\", \"Agent\", \"User\", \"CWD\", \"Created\", \"Updated\"]);\n\n    for s in &sessions {\n        table.add_row([\n            s.session_id.as_str(),\n            &truncate(&s.session_name, 50),\n            s.agent_name.as_str(),\n            s.user.as_str(),\n            s.cwd.as_str(),\n            &s.created_at.format(\"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M\").to_string(),\n            &s.updated_at.format(\"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M\").to_string(),\n        ]);\n    }\n\n    // Render the table first (so comfy-table calculates widths without ANSI codes),\n    // then inject colors around agent names in the output string.\n    println!(\"{}\", colorize_agents(&table.to_string()));\n    Ok(())\n}\n\n// Post-process the rendered table string, wrapping known agent names with ANSI\n// 256-color codes. 256-color is supported by virtually every modern terminal,\n// unlike 24-bit RGB which silently falls back to white on some terminals.\nfn colorize_agents(s: &str) -> String {\n    const AGENTS: &[(&str, u8)] = &[\n        (\"Claude Code\", 214),  // orange\n        (\"Copilot\",     99),   // purple\n        (\"Cursor\",      33),   // blue\n        (\"Gemini\",      75),   // Google blue\n        (\"Aider\",       42),   // green\n        (\"ChatGPT\",     35),   // OpenAI teal-green\n        (\"Windsurf\",    44),   // teal\n        (\"Amazon Q\",    208),  // AWS orange\n    ];\n\n    let mut out = s.to_string();\n    for (name, code) in AGENTS {\n        out = out.replace(name, &format!(\"\\x1b[38;5;{code}m{name}\\x1b[0m\"));\n    }\n    out\n}\n\nfn truncate(s: &str, max: usize) -> String {\n    if s.chars().count() <= max {\n        s.to_string()\n    } else {\n        format!(\"{}…\", s.chars().take(max - 1).collect::<String>())\n    }\n}\n"
}

> AGENT

Clean. Two things changed:

1. **Gray background fixed** — removed `custom_styling` from comfy-table. That feature was injecting cell-level reset codes that appeared as a gray background.

2. **Colors fixed** — instead of `Cell::fg(Color::Rgb {...})` (which silently falls back to white when the terminal doesn't support 24-bit RGB), the table is rendered to a plain string first, then agent names are replaced with ANSI 256-color wrapped versions (`\x1b[38;5;Nm`). 256-color is supported by virtually every modern terminal. Column widths are unaffected because comfy-table calculates them before the ANSI codes are injected.

#!/bin/sh
# Entire CLI hooks
# Post-commit hook: condense session data if commit has Entire-Checkpoint trailer
entire hooks git post-commit 2>/dev/null || true
===
#!/bin/sh
# Entire CLI hooks
# Pre-push hook: push session logs alongside user's push
# $1 is the remote name (e.g., "origin")
entire hooks git pre-push "$1" || true
===
#!/bin/sh
# Entire CLI hooks
# Commit-msg hook: strip trailer if no user content (allows aborting empty commits)
entire hooks git commit-msg "$1" || exit 1
===
#!/bin/sh
# Entire CLI hooks
entire hooks git prepare-commit-msg "$1" "$2" 2>/dev/null || true
```

> AGENT

Now I have the full picture. entireio installs its own git hooks via `entire configure` — specifically `post-commit`, `pre-push`, `commit-msg`, and `prepare-commit-msg`. The worktree at `~/.gossamer/checkpoints/gossamer` is linked back to the main repo's `.git`. So we just need to append to the existing `post-commit` hook after `entire configure` has installed its line there. We check for the `Entire-Checkpoint:` trailer to avoid running on every regular commit.

7	pub async fn run() -> Result<()> {
8	    let cwd = env::current_dir().context("failed to get current directory")?;
9	    let cwd_str = cwd.to_string_lossy().to_string();
10	
11	    let remote = git_remote(&cwd_str)?;
12	    let name = repo_name_from_remote(&remote);
13	
14	    println!("Running `entire configure`...");
15	    let status = Command::new("entire")
16	        .arg("configure")
17	        .status()
18	        .context("`entire configure` failed to launch — is entireio installed?")?;
19	
20	    if !status.success() {
21	        anyhow::bail!("`entire configure` exited with status {}", status);
22	    }
23	
24	    let db = db::connect().await?;
25	
26	    let existing = repository::Entity::find()
27	        .filter(repository::Column::Directory.eq(&cwd_str))
28	        .one(&db)
29	        .await?;
30	
31	    if existing.is_some() {
32	        anyhow::bail!("'{}' is already registered with gossamer", cwd_str);
33	    }
34	
35	    repository::Entity::insert(repository::ActiveModel {
36	        directory: Set(cwd_str.clone()),
37	        remote: Set(remote),
38	        name: Set(name.clone()),
39	        ..Default::default()
40	    })
41	    .exec(&db)
42	    .await
43	    .context("failed to register repository")?;
44	
45	    println!("Initialized '{}' ({})", name, cwd_str);
46	    Ok(())
47	}
48	
49	fn git_remote(dir: &str) -> Result<String> {
50	    let out = Command::new("git")
51	        .args(["remote", "get-url", "origin"])
52	        .current_dir(dir)
53	        .output()
54	        .context("failed to run git")?;
55	
56	    if !out.status.success() {
57	        anyhow::bail!("not a git repository or no 'origin' remote is configured");
58	    }
59	
60	    Ok(String::from_utf8(out.stdout)?.trim().to_string())
61	}
62	
63	fn repo_name_from_remote(remote: &str) -> String {
64	    remote
65	        .rsplit('/')
66	        .next()
67	        .unwrap_or(remote)
68	        .trim_end_matches(".git")
69	        .to_string()
70	}
71

{
  "file_path": "/Users/stephanostsoucas/p/gossamer/src/commands/init.rs",
  "content": "use anyhow::{Context, Result};\nuse sea_orm::{ActiveValue::Set, ColumnTrait, EntityTrait, QueryFilter};\nuse std::{env, fs, io::Write, path::PathBuf, process::Command};\n\nuse crate::{db, entity::repository};\n\n// Marker used to detect an already-installed hook and as the hook body.\nconst HOOK_MARKER: &str = \"# gossamer:\";\nconst HOOK_SNIPPET: &str = r#\"\n# gossamer: re-index sessions after entireio checkpoints\nif git log -1 --format=\"%B\" | grep -q \"Entire-Checkpoint:\"; then\n    gossamer index >/dev/null 2>&1 || true\nfi\"#;\n\npub async fn run() -> Result<()> {\n    let cwd = env::current_dir().context(\"failed to get current directory\")?;\n    let cwd_str = cwd.to_string_lossy().to_string();\n\n    let remote = git_remote(&cwd_str)?;\n    let name = repo_name_from_remote(&remote);\n\n    println!(\"Running `entire configure`...\");\n    let status = Command::new(\"entire\")\n        .arg(\"configure\")\n        .status()\n        .context(\"`entire configure` failed to launch — is entireio installed?\")?;\n\n    if !status.success() {\n        anyhow::bail!(\"`entire configure` exited with status {}\", status);\n    }\n\n    install_post_commit_hook(&cwd_str)?;\n\n    let db = db::connect().await?;\n\n    let existing = repository::Entity::find()\n        .filter(repository::Column::Directory.eq(&cwd_str))\n        .one(&db)\n        .await?;\n\n    if existing.is_some() {\n        anyhow::bail!(\"'{}' is already registered with gossamer\", cwd_str);\n    }\n\n    repository::Entity::insert(repository::ActiveModel {\n        directory: Set(cwd_str.clone()),\n        remote: Set(remote),\n        name: Set(name.clone()),\n        ..Default::default()\n    })\n    .exec(&db)\n    .await\n    .context(\"failed to register repository\")?;\n\n    println!(\"Initialized '{}' ({})\", name, cwd_str);\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nfn install_post_commit_hook(repo_dir: &str) -> Result<()> {\n    // --git-common-dir always returns the real .git directory, even from inside a worktree.\n    let out = Command::new(\"git\")\n        .args([\"rev-parse\", \"--git-common-dir\"])\n        .current_dir(repo_dir)\n        .output()\n        .context(\"failed to run git\")?;\n\n    if !out.status.success() {\n        anyhow::bail!(\"could not locate .git directory\");\n    }\n\n    let git_dir = PathBuf::from(String::from_utf8(out.stdout)?.trim().to_string());\n    let git_dir = if git_dir.is_absolute() {\n        git_dir\n    } else {\n        PathBuf::from(repo_dir).join(git_dir)\n    };\n\n    let hook_path = git_dir.join(\"hooks\").join(\"post-commit\");\n\n    if hook_path.exists() {\n        let content = fs::read_to_string(&hook_path)?;\n        if content.contains(HOOK_MARKER) {\n            println!(\"post-commit hook already contains gossamer indexing, skipping.\");\n            return Ok(());\n        }\n        // Append to the hook entireio already installed.\n        let mut file = fs::OpenOptions::new().append(true).open(&hook_path)?;\n        writeln!(file, \"{}\", HOOK_SNIPPET)?;\n    } else {\n        fs::create_dir_all(hook_path.parent().unwrap())?;\n        fs::write(&hook_path, format!(\"#!/bin/sh\\n{}\\n\", HOOK_SNIPPET))?;\n    }\n\n    // Ensure the hook is executable on Unix.\n    #[cfg(unix)]\n    {\n        use std::os::unix::fs::PermissionsExt;\n        let mut perms = fs::metadata(&hook_path)?.permissions();\n        perms.set_mode(0o755);\n        fs::set_permissions(&hook_path, perms)?;\n    }\n\n    println!(\n        \"Installed post-commit hook at {}\",\n        hook_path.display()\n    );\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nfn git_remote(dir: &str) -> Result<String> {\n    let out = Command::new(\"git\")\n        .args([\"remote\", \"get-url\", \"origin\"])\n        .current_dir(dir)\n        .output()\n        .context(\"failed to run git\")?;\n\n    if !out.status.success() {\n        anyhow::bail!(\"not a git repository or no 'origin' remote is configured\");\n    }\n\n    Ok(String::from_utf8(out.stdout)?.trim().to_string())\n}\n\nfn repo_name_from_remote(remote: &str) -> String {\n    remote\n        .rsplit('/')\n        .next()\n        .unwrap_or(remote)\n        .trim_end_matches(\".git\")\n        .to_string()\n}\n"
}

> AGENT

After `gossamer init` runs, that file will become:

```sh
#!/bin/sh
# Entire CLI hooks
# Post-commit hook: condense session data if commit has Entire-Checkpoint trailer
entire hooks git post-commit 2>/dev/null || true

# gossamer: re-index sessions after entireio checkpoints
if git log -1 --format="%B" | grep -q "Entire-Checkpoint:"; then
    gossamer index >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
fi
```

A few design decisions worth noting:

- Uses `--git-common-dir` instead of `--git-dir` so it works correctly when run from inside a worktree (the hook must live in the shared `.git/hooks/`, not a worktree-local path)
- The `Entire-Checkpoint:` trailer check means the hook is a no-op on every regular commit — only fires when entireio actually wrote a checkpoint
- Idempotent: if the marker is already present it skips silently, so running `gossamer init` twice won't double-append
- Appends to the file entireio already created rather than replacing it, so entireio's line stays first

> DEVELOPER

Would it be better to create a file watcher on the entire checkpoints branch so that we can avoid adding the extra git hook config? i think it would also have the benefit of indexing automatically

> AGENT

It depends on whether gossamer grows into a daemon. A file watcher on `~/.gossamer/checkpoints/` is cleaner architecturally — no hook surgery on repos, works retroactively on any repo, and indexes automatically. The tradeoff is that it requires a long-running `gossamer watch` process to be started (on login or manually), whereas the git hook fires reliably with zero extra process overhead.

If the roadmap includes a TUI or a background server (which seems likely given you started with ratatui), a `gossamer watch` daemon is the right call and the hook can be dropped. If gossamer stays a pure CLI tool invoked by hand, the hook is simpler and more reliable.

My recommendation: keep the hook for now since there's no daemon yet, but design the `index` command to be the single source of truth so switching to a watcher later is just a matter of calling it on `notify` events. The two approaches aren't mutually exclusive — you could ship both and let the hook be a fallback.

1	use anyhow::Result;
2	use chrono::{DateTime, Utc};
3	use comfy_table::{presets::UTF8_FULL, Table};
4	use sea_orm::{EntityTrait, QueryOrder};
5	
6	use crate::{db, entity::session};
7	
8	pub async fn run() -> Result<()> {
9	    let db = db::connect().await?;
10	    let sessions = session::Entity::find()
11	        .order_by_desc(session::Column::UpdatedAt)
12	        .all(&db)
13	        .await?;
14	
15	    if sessions.is_empty() {
16	        println!("No sessions found.");
17	        return Ok(());
18	    }
19	
20	    let mut table = Table::new();
21	    table.load_preset(UTF8_FULL);
22	    table.set_header(["Session ID", "Last Prompt", "Agent", "User", "CWD", "Created", "Updated"]);
23	
24	    for s in &sessions {
25	        table.add_row([
26	            s.session_id.as_str(),
27	            &truncate(&s.session_name, 50),
28	            s.agent_name.as_str(),
29	            s.user.as_str(),
30	            s.cwd.as_str(),
31	            &relative_time(s.created_at),
32	            &relative_time(s.updated_at),
33	        ]);
34	    }
35	
36	    // Render the table first (so comfy-table calculates widths without ANSI codes),
37	    // then inject colors around agent names in the output string.
38	    println!("{}", colorize_agents(&table.to_string()));
39	    Ok(())
40	}
41	
42	// Post-process the rendered table string, wrapping known agent names with ANSI
43	// 256-color codes. 256-color is supported by virtually every modern terminal,
44	// unlike 24-bit RGB which silently falls back to white on some terminals.
45	fn colorize_agents(s: &str) -> String {
46	    const AGENTS: &[(&str, u8)] = &[
47	        ("Claude Code", 214),  // orange
48	        ("Copilot",     99),   // purple
49	        ("Cursor",      33),   // blue
50	        ("Gemini",      75),   // Google blue
51	        ("Aider",       42),   // green
52	        ("ChatGPT",     35),   // OpenAI teal-green
53	        ("Windsurf",    44),   // teal
54	        ("Amazon Q",    208),  // AWS orange
55	    ];
56	
57	    let mut out = s.to_string();
58	    for (name, code) in AGENTS {
59	        out = out.replace(name, &format!("\x1b[38;5;{code}m{name}\x1b[0m"));
60	    }
61	    out
62	}
63	
64	fn relative_time(dt: DateTime<Utc>) -> String {
65	    let secs = (Utc::now() - dt).num_seconds();
66	    match secs {
67	        s if s < 60 => "just now".to_string(),
68	        s if s < 3_600 => format!("{} min{} ago", s / 60, if s / 60 == 1 { "" } else { "s" }),

72	        s if s < 31_536_000 => format!("{} mo ago", s / 2_592_000),
73	        s => format!("{} yr{} ago", s / 31_536_000, if s / 31_536_000 == 1 { "" } else { "s" }),
74	    }
75	}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/stephanostsoucas/p/gossamer/src/commands/sessions.rs",
  "content": "use anyhow::Result;\nuse chrono::{DateTime, Utc};\nuse comfy_table::{presets::UTF8_FULL, Table};\nuse sea_orm::{EntityTrait, QueryOrder};\n\nuse crate::{db, entity::session};\n\npub async fn run() -> Result<()> {\n    let db = db::connect().await?;\n    let sessions = session::Entity::find()\n        .order_by_desc(session::Column::UpdatedAt)\n        .all(&db)\n        .await?;\n\n    if sessions.is_empty() {\n        println!(\"No sessions found.\");\n        return Ok(());\n    }\n\n    let now = Utc::now();\n    let mut table = Table::new();\n    table.load_preset(UTF8_FULL);\n    table.set_header([\"\", \"Session ID\", \"Last Prompt\", \"Agent\", \"User\", \"CWD\", \"Created\", \"Updated\"]);\n\n    for s in &sessions {\n        let age = (now - s.updated_at).num_seconds();\n        let dot = if age < 3_600 { \"● \" } else if age < 86_400 { \"● \" } else { \"\" };\n\n        table.add_row([\n            dot,\n            s.session_id.as_str(),\n            &truncate(&s.session_name, 50),\n            s.agent_name.as_str(),\n            s.user.as_str(),\n            s.cwd.as_str(),\n            &relative_time(s.created_at),\n            &relative_time(s.updated_at),\n        ]);\n    }\n\n    println!(\"{}\", colorize(&table.to_string(), &sessions, now));\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nfn colorize(s: &str, sessions: &[session::Model], now: DateTime<Utc>) -> String {\n    const AGENTS: &[(&str, u8)] = &[\n        (\"Claude Code\", 214),\n        (\"Copilot\",     99),\n        (\"Cursor\",      33),\n        (\"Gemini\",      75),\n        (\"Aider\",       42),\n        (\"ChatGPT\",     35),\n        (\"Windsurf\",    44),\n        (\"Amazon Q\",    208),\n    ];\n\n    let mut out = s.to_string();\n\n    // Agent name colors\n    for (name, code) in AGENTS {\n        out = out.replace(name, &format!(\"\\x1b[38;5;{code}m{name}\\x1b[0m\"));\n    }\n\n    // Activity dots — the dot is already in the rendered string for correct\n    // column width; we just wrap it in the right ANSI color here.\n    for session in sessions {\n        let age = (now - session.updated_at).num_seconds();\n        let (dot, code) = if age < 3_600 {\n            (\"● \", 82)   // green\n        } else if age < 86_400 {\n            (\"● \", 214)  // amber\n        } else {\n            continue;\n        };\n        out = out.replacen(dot, &format!(\"\\x1b[38;5;{code}m●\\x1b[0m \"), 1);\n    }\n\n    out\n}\n\nfn relative_time(dt: DateTime<Utc>) -> String {\n    let secs = (Utc::now() - dt).num_seconds();\n    match secs {\n        s if s < 60 => \"just now\".to_string(),\n        s if s < 3_600 => format!(\"{} min{} ago\", s / 60, if s / 60 == 1 { \"\" } else { \"s\" }),\n        s if s < 86_400 => format!(\"{} hr{} ago\", s / 3_600, if s / 3_600 == 1 { \"\" } else { \"s\" }),\n        s if s < 604_800 => format!(\"{} day{} ago\", s / 86_400, if s / 86_400 == 1 { \"\" } else { \"s\" }),\n        s if s < 2_592_000 => format!(\"{} wk{} ago\", s / 604_800, if s / 604_800 == 1 { \"\" } else { \"s\" }),\n        s if s < 31_536_000 => format!(\"{} mo ago\", s / 2_592_000),\n        s => format!(\"{} yr{} ago\", s / 31_536_000, if s / 31_536_000 == 1 { \"\" } else { \"s\" }),\n    }\n}\n\nfn truncate(s: &str, max: usize) -> String {\n    if s.chars().count() <= max {\n        s.to_string()\n    } else {\n        format!(\"{}…\", s.chars().take(max - 1).collect::<String>())\n    }\n}\n"
}

> AGENT

Done. A few notes on the approach:

- The dot goes in a dedicated narrow first column (header `""`) so the Session ID column keeps a consistent width regardless of whether a session has a dot or not — no ragged alignment.
- The dot `●` is placed in the cell as plain text first so comfy-table measures the column width correctly, then `colorize()` wraps it in ANSI color as a post-processing step.
- Green (`\x1b[38;5;82m`) for < 1 hour, amber (`\x1b[38;5;214m`) for < 24 hours, no dot for older sessions.
- `replacen(..., 1)` ensures only the first occurrence of `●` is colored per pass, preventing a session's dot from accidentally matching a later row's dot.
