{
  "file_path": "/Users/stephanostsoucas/p/gossamer/src/commands/init.rs",
  "content": "use anyhow::{Context, Result};\nuse sea_orm::{ActiveValue::Set, ColumnTrait, EntityTrait, QueryFilter};\nuse std::{env, fs, io::Write, path::PathBuf, process::Command};\n\nuse crate::{db, entity::repository};\n\nconst HOOK_MARKER: &str = \"# gossamer:\";\nconst HOOK_SNIPPET: &str = r#\"\n# gossamer: re-index sessions after entireio checkpoints\nif git log -1 --format=\"%B\" | grep -q \"Entire-Checkpoint:\"; then\n    gossamer index >/dev/null 2>&1 || true\nfi\"#;\n\npub async fn run() -> Result<()> {\n    let cwd = env::current_dir().context(\"failed to get current directory\")?;\n    let cwd_str = cwd.to_string_lossy().to_string();\n\n    let remote = git_remote(&cwd_str)?;\n    let name = repo_name_from_remote(&remote);\n\n    if entire_already_configured(&cwd_str) {\n        println!(\"`entire` hooks already installed, skipping `entire configure`.\");\n    } else {\n        println!(\"Running `entire configure`...\");\n        let status = Command::new(\"entire\")\n            .arg(\"configure\")\n            .status()\n            .context(\"`entire configure` failed to launch — is entireio installed?\")?;\n\n        if !status.success() {\n            anyhow::bail!(\"`entire configure` exited with status {}\", status);\n        }\n    }\n\n    install_post_commit_hook(&cwd_str)?;\n\n    let db = db::connect().await?;\n\n    let existing = repository::Entity::find()\n        .filter(repository::Column::Directory.eq(&cwd_str))\n        .one(&db)\n        .await?;\n\n    if existing.is_some() {\n        println!(\"'{}' is already registered with gossamer.\", name);\n        return Ok(());\n    }\n\n    repository::Entity::insert(repository::ActiveModel {\n        directory: Set(cwd_str.clone()),\n        remote: Set(remote),\n        name: Set(name.clone()),\n        ..Default::default()\n    })\n    .exec(&db)\n    .await\n    .context(\"failed to register repository\")?;\n\n    println!(\"Initialized '{}' ({}).\", name, cwd_str);\n    Ok(())\n}\n\n/// Returns true if entireio has already written its hooks into this repo.\nfn entire_already_configured(repo_dir: &str) -> bool {\n    let git_dir = match git_common_dir(repo_dir) {\n        Ok(d) => d,\n        Err(_) => return false,\n    };\n    let post_commit = git_dir.join(\"hooks\").join(\"post-commit\");\n    fs::read_to_string(post_commit)\n        .map(|c| c.contains(\"entire hooks git\"))\n        .unwrap_or(false)\n}\n\nfn install_post_commit_hook(repo_dir: &str) -> Result<()> {\n    let git_dir = git_common_dir(repo_dir)?;\n    let hook_path = git_dir.join(\"hooks\").join(\"post-commit\");\n\n    if hook_path.exists() {\n        let content = fs::read_to_string(&hook_path)?;\n        if content.contains(HOOK_MARKER) {\n            println!(\"post-commit hook already contains gossamer indexing, skipping.\");\n            return Ok(());\n        }\n        let mut file = fs::OpenOptions::new().append(true).open(&hook_path)?;\n        writeln!(file, \"{}\", HOOK_SNIPPET)?;\n    } else {\n        fs::create_dir_all(hook_path.parent().unwrap())?;\n        fs::write(&hook_path, format!(\"#!/bin/sh\\n{}\\n\", HOOK_SNIPPET))?;\n    }\n\n    #[cfg(unix)]\n    {\n        use std::os::unix::fs::PermissionsExt;\n        let mut perms = fs::metadata(&hook_path)?.permissions();\n        perms.set_mode(0o755);\n        fs::set_permissions(&hook_path, perms)?;\n    }\n\n    println!(\"Installed post-commit hook at {}.\", hook_path.display());\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nfn git_common_dir(repo_dir: &str) -> Result<PathBuf> {\n    let out = Command::new(\"git\")\n        .args([\"rev-parse\", \"--git-common-dir\"])\n        .current_dir(repo_dir)\n        .output()\n        .context(\"failed to run git\")?;\n\n    if !out.status.success() {\n        anyhow::bail!(\"could not locate .git directory\");\n    }\n\n    let rel = PathBuf::from(String::from_utf8(out.stdout)?.trim().to_string());\n    Ok(if rel.is_absolute() {\n        rel\n    } else {\n        PathBuf::from(repo_dir).join(rel)\n    })\n}\n\nfn git_remote(dir: &str) -> Result<String> {\n    let out = Command::new(\"git\")\n        .args([\"remote\", \"get-url\", \"origin\"])\n        .current_dir(dir)\n        .output()\n        .context(\"failed to run git\")?;\n\n    if !out.status.success() {\n        anyhow::bail!(\"not a git repository or no 'origin' remote is configured\");\n    }\n\n    Ok(String::from_utf8(out.stdout)?.trim().to_string())\n}\n\nfn repo_name_from_remote(remote: &str) -> String {\n    remote\n        .rsplit('/')\n        .next()\n        .unwrap_or(remote)\n        .trim_end_matches(\".git\")\n        .to_string()\n}\n"
}

#!/bin/sh
# Entire CLI hooks
# Post-commit hook: condense session data if commit has Entire-Checkpoint trailer
entire hooks git post-commit 2>/dev/null || true

# gossamer: re-index sessions after entireio checkpoints
if git log -1 --format="%B" | grep -q "Entire-Checkpoint:"; then
    gossamer index >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
fi
```

> AGENT

Hook is installed and `init` is fully idempotent — running it twice produces the same result. The three independent checks each skip gracefully:

1. **entireio already configured** — detects `entire hooks git` in the existing post-commit hook, skips `entire configure`
2. **gossamer hook already present** — detects the `# gossamer:` marker, skips appending
3. **repo already registered** — finds the directory in the DB, skips the insert

The most recent checkpoint is from April 28 — 5 days ago. The current live session hasn't been committed as a checkpoint yet (that's normal — entireio only writes on commit). > AGENT

`gossamer` is now installed and the hook will fire correctly on future checkpoint commits. The current session genuinely isn't in the checkpoints yet — entireio only writes a checkpoint on commit (with the `Entire-Checkpoint:` trailer), so any live session won't appear until you make your next commit. That's expected — the "4 days ago" session is the last committed checkpoint.

Two things to note from the output:
- The CWD on those sessions is `/Users/stsoucas/cosmos/p/gossamer` — a different path, suggesting you were working on the old gossamer from a different machine/mount. The current session at `/Users/stephanostsoucas/p/gossamer` will appear as its own entry after your next checkpoint commit.
- The `gossamer-cli` checkpoint directory doesn't exist yet — entireio creates it on the first checkpoint commit from this project.

> AGENT

The root issue is clear: our indexer looks for a worktree at `~/.gossamer/checkpoints/<name>/` but the source of truth is the `entire/checkpoints/v1` branch in each registered repo's git. The worktree was a coincidence for the old gossamer project. We need to read directly from the branch.

> TOOL

tool_use Write
id: toolu_01AzZGx8NnLMkxV87aFPe3on
```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/stephanostsoucas/p/gossamer/src/commands/index.rs",
  "content": "use anyhow::{Context, Result};\nuse chrono::{DateTime, Utc};\nuse sea_orm::{sea_query::OnConflict, ActiveValue::Set, DatabaseConnection, EntityTrait};\nuse serde::Deserialize;\nuse serde_json::Value;\nuse std::process::Command;\n\nuse crate::{\n    db,\n    entity::{repository, session},\n};\n\nconst BRANCH: &str = \"entire/checkpoints/v1\";\n\npub async fn run() -> Result<()> {\n    let db = db::connect().await?;\n    let repos = repository::Entity::find().all(&db).await?;\n\n    if repos.is_empty() {\n        println!(\"No repositories tracked. Run `gossamer init` first.\");\n        return Ok(());\n    }\n\n    let mut grand_total = 0usize;\n\n    for repo in &repos {\n        match index_repo(&db, &repo.directory, &repo.name).await {\n            Ok(0) => println!(\"'{}': no {} branch found.\", repo.name, BRANCH),\n            Ok(n) => {\n                println!(\"'{}': indexed {} session(s).\", repo.name, n);\n                grand_total += n;\n            }\n            Err(e) => eprintln!(\"'{}': error — {}\", repo.name, e),\n        }\n    }\n\n    println!(\"\\n{} session(s) indexed.\", grand_total);\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nasync fn index_repo(db: &DatabaseConnection, repo_dir: &str, _repo_name: &str) -> Result<usize> {\n    // Bail early if the branch doesn't exist in this repo.\n    let check = Command::new(\"git\")\n        .args([\"rev-parse\", \"--verify\", BRANCH])\n        .current_dir(repo_dir)\n        .output()\n        .context(\"failed to run git\")?;\n\n    if !check.status.success() {\n        return Ok(0);\n    }\n\n    // List every file tracked on the branch.\n    let ls = Command::new(\"git\")\n        .args([\"ls-tree\", \"-r\", \"--name-only\", BRANCH])\n        .current_dir(repo_dir)\n        .output()\n        .context(\"git ls-tree failed\")?;\n\n    let listing = String::from_utf8(ls.stdout)?;\n\n    // Session metadata lives at XX/yyyyyyyyyy/N/metadata.json where N is all digits.\n    let meta_paths: Vec<&str> = listing\n        .lines()\n        .filter(|l| {\n            l.ends_with(\"/metadata.json\")\n                && l.matches('/').count() == 3\n                && l.split('/').nth(2).map_or(false, |s| s.chars().all(|c| c.is_ascii_digit()))\n        })\n        .collect();\n\n    let user = std::env::var(\"USER\")\n        .or_else(|_| std::env::var(\"USERNAME\"))\n        .unwrap_or_else(|_| \"unknown\".to_string());\n\n    let mut count = 0;\n\n    for meta_path in meta_paths {\n        let jsonl_path = format!(\"{}full.jsonl\", &meta_path[..meta_path.len() - \"metadata.json\".len()]);\n\n        let meta_bytes = match git_show(repo_dir, meta_path) {\n            Ok(b) => b,\n            Err(e) => { eprintln!(\"  skipping {}: {}\", meta_path, e); continue; }\n        };\n        let jsonl_bytes = match git_show(repo_dir, &jsonl_path) {\n            Ok(b) => b,\n            Err(_) => continue,\n        };\n\n        match parse_session(&meta_bytes, &jsonl_bytes, &user) {\n            Ok(model) => {\n                upsert_session(db, model).await?;\n                count += 1;\n            }\n            Err(e) => eprintln!(\"  skipping {}: {}\", meta_path, e),\n        }\n    }\n\n    Ok(count)\n}\n\nfn git_show(repo_dir: &str, path: &str) -> Result<Vec<u8>> {\n    let out = Command::new(\"git\")\n        .args([\"show\", &format!(\"{}:{}\", BRANCH, path)])\n        .current_dir(repo_dir)\n        .output()\n        .context(\"failed to run git show\")?;\n\n    if !out.status.success() {\n        anyhow::bail!(\"object not found: {}\", path);\n    }\n    Ok(out.stdout)\n}\n\nfn parse_session(meta_bytes: &[u8], jsonl_bytes: &[u8], user: &str) -> Result<session::ActiveModel> {\n    let meta: SessionMetadata = serde_json::from_slice(meta_bytes)\n        .context(\"failed to parse metadata.json\")?;\n\n    let created_at = meta\n        .created_at\n        .as_deref()\n        .and_then(|s| DateTime::parse_from_rfc3339(s).ok())\n        .map(|dt: DateTime<chrono::FixedOffset>| dt.with_timezone(&Utc))\n        .unwrap_or_else(Utc::now);\n\n    let session_name = meta\n        .summary\n        .and_then(|s| s.intent)\n        .filter(|s| !s.is_empty())\n        .unwrap_or_else(|| format!(\"session:{}\", &meta.session_id[..8]));\n\n    let agent_name = meta.agent.unwrap_or_else(|| \"unknown\".to_string());\n\n    let mut latest: Option<DateTime<Utc>> = None;\n    let mut cwd = String::new();\n    let mut last_prompt: Option<String> = None;\n\n    for line in jsonl_bytes.split(|&b| b == b'\\n') {\n        if line.is_empty() {\n            continue;\n        }\n        let v: Value = match serde_json::from_slice(line) {\n            Ok(v) => v,\n            Err(_) => continue,\n        };\n\n        if let Some(ts) = v.get(\"timestamp\").and_then(Value::as_str) {\n            if let Ok(dt) = DateTime::parse_from_rfc3339(ts) {\n                let dt: DateTime<Utc> = dt.with_timezone(&Utc);\n                if latest.map_or(true, |l| dt > l) {\n                    latest = Some(dt);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        if cwd.is_empty() {\n            if let Some(c) = v.get(\"cwd\").and_then(Value::as_str) {\n                cwd = c.to_string();\n            }\n        }\n\n        if v.get(\"type\").and_then(Value::as_str) == Some(\"user\") {\n            if let Some(Value::String(text)) = v.get(\"message\").and_then(|m| m.get(\"content\")) {\n                let text = text.trim().to_string();\n                if !text.is_empty() {\n                    last_prompt = Some(text);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    let updated_at = latest.unwrap_or(created_at);\n    let session_name = last_prompt.unwrap_or(session_name);\n\n    Ok(session::ActiveModel {\n        session_id: Set(meta.session_id),\n        agent_name: Set(agent_name),\n        user: Set(user.to_string()),\n        created_at: Set(created_at),\n        updated_at: Set(updated_at),\n        cwd: Set(cwd),\n        session_name: Set(session_name),\n    })\n}\n\nasync fn upsert_session(db: &DatabaseConnection, model: session::ActiveModel) -> Result<()> {\n    session::Entity::insert(model)\n        .on_conflict(\n            OnConflict::column(session::Column::SessionId)\n                .update_columns([\n                    session::Column::AgentName,\n                    session::Column::UpdatedAt,\n                    session::Column::Cwd,\n                    session::Column::SessionName,\n                ])\n                .to_owned(),\n        )\n        .exec(db)\n        .await?;\n    Ok(())\n}\n\n#[derive(Deserialize)]\nstruct SessionMetadata {\n    session_id: String,\n    agent: Option<String>,\n    created_at: Option<String>,\n    summary: Option<Summary>,\n}\n\n#[derive(Deserialize)]\nstruct Summary {\n    intent: Option<String>,\n}\n"
}

id: toolu_011d65q6YcGVJvGh4FyU6YF4
```
Perfect! Now I have all the detailed information. Let me compile the complete answer with the exact JSON schema for the user.

Based on the official Claude Code documentation, here is the exact JSON schema for configuring hooks in settings.json:

## 1. **Complete Hooks Configuration Structure**

```json
{
  "hooks": {
    "EventName": [
      {
        "matcher": "filter_pattern",
        "hooks": [
          {
            "type": "command|http|mcp_tool|prompt|agent",
            "command": "script_path_or_command",
            "if": "permission_rule_pattern",
            "timeout": 600,
            "statusMessage": "Custom spinner message",
            "once": false,
            "async": false,
            "asyncRewake": false
          }
        ]
      }
    ]
  }
}
```

## 2. **SessionStart Hook - Event Name & Exact Structure**

Yes, **`SessionStart` is the exact event name**. It has no alternative. Here's the precise configuration:

```json
{
  "hooks": {
    "SessionStart": [
      {
        "matcher": "startup|resume|clear|compact",
        "hooks": [
          {
            "type": "command",
            "command": "/path/to/script.sh"
          }
        ]
      }
    ]
  }
}
```

**Matcher values for SessionStart:**
- `"startup"` - New session
- `"resume"` - `--resume`, `--continue`, or `/resume`
- `"clear"` - `/clear`
- `"compact"` - Auto or manual compaction
- `""` (empty) - All session starts (no filtering)

## 3. **Hook Input/Output - Environment Variables & stdin/stdout**

### stdin (Hook Input)
Hooks receive JSON on stdin with these **common fields** for all events:
```json
{
  "session_id": "abc123",
  "cwd": "/current/working/directory",
  "hook_event_name": "SessionStart",
  "transcript_path": "/path/to/transcript.jsonl"
}
```

### SessionStart-Specific stdin Fields
```json
{
  "session_id": "abc123",
  "cwd": "/Users/...",
  "hook_event_name": "SessionStart",
  "transcript_path": "/Users/.../.claude/projects/.../00893aaf-19fa-41d2-8238-13269b9b3ca0.jsonl",
  "source": "startup|resume|clear|compact",
  "model": "claude-sonnet-4-6",
  "agent_type": "optional_agent_name"
}
```

### Environment Variables Available to Hooks
```bash
$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR         # Project root directory
${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}       # Plugin installation directory
${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA}       # Plugin persistent data directory
$CLAUDE_ENV_FILE            # (SessionStart/Setup only) Write exports here
$CLAUDE_CODE_REMOTE         # "true" in web, unset in CLI
```

### stdout & Exit Codes
- **Exit 0** = Success. Any stdout text is added to Claude's context
- **Exit 2** = Blocking error (for events that support it). Write error reason to stderr
- **Other codes** = Non-blocking error. First line of stderr shows in transcript

### stdout JSON Format for SessionStart
```json
{
  "continue": true,
  "suppressOutput": false,
  "systemMessage": "Optional warning to user",
  "hookSpecificOutput": {
    "hookEventName": "SessionStart",
    "additionalContext": "Text to inject into Claude's context"
  }
}
```

### CLAUDE_ENV_FILE (SessionStart Only)
Write shell exports to this file to persist environment variables across Bash commands:
```bash
#!/bin/bash
if [ -n "$CLAUDE_ENV_FILE" ]; then
  echo 'export NODE_ENV=production' >> "$CLAUDE_ENV_FILE"
  echo 'export DEBUG_LOG=true' >> "$CLAUDE_ENV_FILE"
fi
exit 0
```

## 4. **Hook Scoping - Global vs Project**

Hooks can be scoped by location, **but NOT by individual project directory**:

| Location | Scope | Shareable |
|----------|-------|-----------|
| `~/.claude/settings.json` | **All projects** (global) | No, local to machine |
| `.claude/settings.json` | **Single project** (in repo root) | Yes, can commit |
| `.claude/settings.local.json` | **Single project** | No, gitignored |
| `.claude/settings.json` + `.claude/settings.local.json` | Merged (local overrides) | Local overrides only |
| Managed settings | Organization-wide | Admin-controlled |
| Plugin `hooks/hooks.json` | When plugin enabled | Bundled with plugin |

**Note:** There is **no per-directory scoping within a project**. Hooks in `.claude/settings.json` apply to the entire project regardless of subdirectory.

## 5. **Minimal SessionStart Hook Example**

```json
{
  "hooks": {
    "SessionStart": [
      {
        "matcher": "",
        "hooks": [
          {
            "type": "command",
            "command": "echo 'Session starting in '$(pwd)' with model: '$CLAUDE_MODEL"
          }
        ]
      }
    ]
  }
}
```

Or with a script file:
```json
{
  "hooks": {
    "SessionStart": [
      {
        "matcher": "startup",
        "hooks": [
          {
            "type": "command",
            "command": "\"$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR\"/.claude/hooks/init-session.sh"
          }
        ]
      }
    ]
  }
}
```

### Script file (`.claude/hooks/init-session.sh`):
```bash
#!/bin/bash
INPUT=$(cat)
SOURCE=$(echo "$INPUT" | jq -r '.source')
CWD=$(echo "$INPUT" | jq -r '.cwd')

# Add context
echo "Starting session from: $SOURCE in $CWD"
exit 0
```

## Key Facts

1. **SessionStart is the exact event name** - no alternatives
2. **Hooks are NOT project-directory-scoped** - only file-location scoped
3. **stdin provides JSON** with `session_id`, `cwd`, `source`, `model`, etc.
4. **stdout feeds directly to Claude** as context (no JSON wrapper needed for plain text)
5. **$CLAUDE_ENV_FILE** is exclusive to SessionStart/Setup for persisting environment variables
6. **Exit code 0** = allow/proceed, **exit code 2** = block (where supported)
7. **Your configuration already has SessionStart working** - see your `.claude/settings.json` which includes `"matcher": ""` and runs on all session starts

For the complete reference including all 20+ hook events and advanced features: https://code.claude.com/docs/en/hooks.md

All idempotent. Here's what was added:

- **`gossamer session-start`** (hidden command): reads Claude Code's SessionStart JSON from stdin, inserts a row with `session_id`, `cwd`, agent `"Claude Code"`, and the current time. Uses `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING` so it won't overwrite sessions already indexed from `entire/checkpoints/v1`.
- **`gossamer init`** now registers the hook in `~/.claude/settings.json` with `matcher: ""` (fires on all session starts — startup, resume, clear, compact).

The hook fires immediately on the next Claude Code session start in any directory.

Are we handling the case where the checkpoints are being saved to a different repo?

> AGENT

Looking at the current code, there are two gaps:

1. **`gossamer index`** only scans repos explicitly registered via `gossamer init`. If a Claude Code session runs in a repo that hasn't been `init`-ed, the `session_start` hook creates a placeholder row but `gossamer index` never fills in the checkpoint data for it.

2. **More specifically**: entireio may store checkpoints for repo A's sessions on the `entire/checkpoints/v1` branch of a *different* repo (e.g., a dedicated remote checkpoint repo). Our indexer runs `git ls-tree` in each registered repo's own directory — so if the checkpoints live elsewhere, we'd miss them entirely.

Looking at the working case: we indexed 190 sessions from the gossamer repo because its own `entire/checkpoints/v1` branch contains those sessions. But do you know if entireio ever stores checkpoints in a separate/remote repo, or is the branch always in the same repo as the project?

That distinction determines the fix:
- If it's always the same repo → the only gap is unregistered repos (easy: auto-register on `session_start` using the `cwd`)  
- If there's a separate checkpoint repo → we need a way to discover or register it separately

# Checkpoint Remote Feature

## What It Is

The checkpoint remote feature allows you to push checkpoint data to a separate repository instead of storing it alongside your code. As stated in the documentation, this is "useful for keeping checkpoint data off your main repository on GitHub, centralizing checkpoints across multiple repositories, or separating audit trails from production code."

## Configuration

You can set up a checkpoint remote in two ways:

1. **Via CLI command:**
   ```bash
   entire enable --checkpoint-remote github:myorg/checkpoints-private
   ```

2. **Via settings file** by adding provider and repository details to `.entire/settings.json`

Currently, only GitHub is supported as a provider.

## Where Checkpoints Are Stored

When configured, the `entire/checkpoints/v1` branch is pushed to your designated checkpoint remote rather than your main repository. Entire "automatically derives the checkpoint URL" using the same protocol (SSH or HTTPS) as your code push remote.

## Remote Repository Structure

The documentation doesn't explicitly detail the internal structure of the checkpoint remote repository itself. It indicates that checkpoint data is organized in a `entire/checkpoints/v1` branch containing "session logs and other metadata," but specifics about directory organization or file layouts within that remote aren't provided.

## Safety Features

The system includes fork detection: if your push remote owner differs from the checkpoint remote owner, checkpoints skip syncing to prevent accidental writes to upstream private repositories.
```

# Checkpoint Remote Configuration

## .entire/settings.json Structure

Based on the documentation, when a checkpoint remote is configured, the JSON structure looks like this:

```json
{
  "strategy_options": {
    "checkpoint_remote": {
      "provider": "github",
      "repo": "myorg/checkpoints-private"
    }
  }
}
```

The file contains a `strategy_options` object with a `checkpoint_remote` field that includes:
- **provider**: Currently only `"github"` is supported
- **repo**: The organization and repository name in `owner/repo` format

## CLI Query Capability

The documentation provided does not include information about querying the checkpoint remote through the Entire CLI. The guide covers configuration via the `entire enable --checkpoint-remote` command but doesn't describe any CLI commands for inspecting or querying checkpoint remote data.

For that functionality, you would need to consult additional documentation or contact Entire's support team.

> AGENT

Neither of the gossamer repos has a checkpoint remote, but the structure is clear: `strategy_options.checkpoint_remote.repo` holds `"owner/repo"`. The fix is: in `index_repo`, read `.entire/settings.json`, and if a checkpoint remote is configured, `git fetch` that remote's `entire/checkpoints/v1` into the local repo before scanning — mirroring the SSH/HTTPS protocol from the project's own origin remote.

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/stephanostsoucas/p/gossamer/src/commands/index.rs",
  "old_string": "fn parse_session(meta_bytes: &[u8], jsonl_bytes: &[u8], user: &str) -> Result<session::ActiveModel> {",
  "new_string": "/// Reads .entire/settings.json and returns a git URL for the checkpoint remote if configured.\n/// Mirrors the SSH/HTTPS protocol of the project's own origin remote.\nfn checkpoint_remote_url(repo_dir: &str) -> Option<String> {\n    #[derive(Deserialize)]\n    struct EntireSettings {\n        strategy_options: Option<StrategyOptions>,\n    }\n    #[derive(Deserialize)]\n    struct StrategyOptions {\n        checkpoint_remote: Option<CheckpointRemote>,\n    }\n    #[derive(Deserialize)]\n    struct CheckpointRemote {\n        repo: String,\n    }\n\n    let settings_path = Path::new(repo_dir).join(\".entire\").join(\"settings.json\");\n    let raw = std::fs::read_to_string(settings_path).ok()?;\n    let settings: EntireSettings = serde_json::from_str(&raw).ok()?;\n    let cp_repo = settings.strategy_options?.checkpoint_remote?.repo;\n\n    // Derive the URL protocol from the project's own origin remote.\n    let use_ssh = Command::new(\"git\")\n        .args([\"remote\", \"get-url\", \"origin\"])\n        .current_dir(repo_dir)\n        .output()\n        .ok()\n        .and_then(|o| String::from_utf8(o.stdout).ok())\n        .map(|u| u.trim_start().starts_with(\"git@\") || u.trim_start().starts_with(\"ssh://\"))\n        .unwrap_or(true);\n\n    let url = if use_ssh {\n        format!(\"git@github.com:{}.git\", cp_repo)\n    } else {\n        format!(\"https://github.com/{}.git\", cp_repo)\n    };\n\n    Some(url)\n}\n\nfn parse_session(meta_bytes: &[u8], jsonl_bytes: &[u8], user: &str) -> Result<session::ActiveModel> {"
}

> AGENT

The behavior now: before running `git ls-tree`, `index_repo` reads `.entire/settings.json` and if `strategy_options.checkpoint_remote.repo` is set, it runs `git fetch git@github.com:<owner>/<repo>.git entire/checkpoints/v1:entire/checkpoints/v1` (SSH or HTTPS depending on the project's own origin). The fetched branch then lands in the local repo's object store and the existing `ls-tree`/`show` logic picks it up unchanged.

Two notes on the current approach worth flagging:
- If two registered repos share the same checkpoint remote, they'll both fetch and scan it — harmless since the upsert is idempotent, but redundant work
- The fetch is silent-fail (`let _ = ...`) so a missing network or bad remote just falls through to the local branch check

1	use anyhow::Result;
2	use chrono::{DateTime, Utc};
3	use comfy_table::{presets::UTF8_HORIZONTAL_ONLY, Table};
4	use sea_orm::{EntityTrait, QueryOrder};
5	
6	use crate::{db, entity::session};
7	
8	pub async fn run(all: bool) -> Result<()> {
9	    let db = db::connect().await?;
10	    let sessions = session::Entity::find()
11	        .order_by_desc(session::Column::UpdatedAt)
12	        .all(&db)
13	        .await?;
14	
15	    let cutoff = Utc::now() - chrono::Duration::days(3);
16	    let sessions: Vec<_> = if all {
17	        sessions
18	    } else {
19	        sessions.into_iter().filter(|s| s.updated_at >= cutoff).collect()
20	    };
21	
22	    if sessions.is_empty() {
23	        println!("No sessions found.");
24	        return Ok(());
25	    }
26	
27	    let now = Utc::now();
28	    let mut table = Table::new();
29	    table.load_preset(UTF8_HORIZONTAL_ONLY);
30	    table.set_header(["", "Session ID", "Last Prompt", "Agent", "User", "CWD", "Created", "Updated"]);
31	
32	    for s in &sessions {
33	        let age = (now - s.updated_at).num_seconds();
34	        let dot = if age < 3_600 { "● " } else if age < 86_400 { "● " } else { "" };
35	
36	        table.add_row([
37	            dot,
38	            s.session_id.as_str(),
39	            &truncate(&s.session_name, 50),
40	            s.agent_name.as_str(),
41	            s.user.as_str(),
42	            s.cwd.as_str(),
43	            &relative_time(s.created_at),
44	            &relative_time(s.updated_at),
45	        ]);
46	    }
47	
48	    println!("{}", colorize(&table.to_string(), &sessions, now));
49	    Ok(())
50	}
51	
52	fn colorize(s: &str, sessions: &[session::Model], now: DateTime<Utc>) -> String {
53	    const AGENTS: &[(&str, u8)] = &[
54	        ("Claude Code", 214),
55	        ("Copilot",     99),
56	        ("Cursor",      33),
57	        ("Gemini",      75),
58	        ("Aider",       42),
59	        ("ChatGPT",     35),
60	        ("Windsurf",    44),
61	        ("Amazon Q",    208),
62	    ];
63	
64	    let mut out = s.to_string();
65	
66	    // Agent name colors
67	    for (name, code) in AGENTS {
68	        out = out.replace(name, &format!("\x1b[38;5;{code}m{name}\x1b[0m"));
69	    }
70	
71	    // Activity dots — the dot is already in the rendered string for correct
72	    // column width; we just wrap it in the right ANSI color here.
73	    for session in sessions {
74	        let age = (now - session.updated_at).num_seconds();
75	        let (dot, code) = if age < 3_600 {
76	            ("● ", 82)   // green
77	        } else if age < 86_400 {
78	            ("● ", 214)  // amber
79	        } else {
80	            continue;
81	        };
82	        out = out.replacen(dot, &format!("\x1b[38;5;{code}m●\x1b[0m "), 1);
83	    }
84	
85	    out
86	}
87	
88	fn relative_time(dt: DateTime<Utc>) -> String {
89	    let secs = (Utc::now() - dt).num_seconds();
90	    match secs {
91	        s if s < 60 => "just now".to_string(),
92	        s if s < 3_600 => format!("{} min{} ago", s / 60, if s / 60 == 1 { "" } else { "s" }),
93	        s if s < 86_400 => format!("{} hr{} ago", s / 3_600, if s / 3_600 == 1 { "" } else { "s" }),
94	        s if s < 604_800 => format!("{} day{} ago", s / 86_400, if s / 86_400 == 1 { "" } else { "s" }),
95	        s if s < 2_592_000 => format!("{} wk{} ago", s / 604_800, if s / 604_800 == 1 { "" } else { "s" }),
96	        s if s < 31_536_000 => format!("{} mo ago", s / 2_592_000),
97	        s => format!("{} yr{} ago", s / 31_536_000, if s / 31_536_000 == 1 { "" } else { "s" }),
98	    }
99	}
100	
101	fn truncate(s: &str, max: usize) -> String {
102	    if s.chars().count() <= max {
103	        s.to_string()
104	    } else {
105	        format!("{}…", s.chars().take(max - 1).collect::<String>())
106	    }
107	}
108	
```

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/stephanostsoucas/p/gossamer/src/commands/status.rs",
  "content": "use anyhow::Result;\nuse comfy_table::{presets::UTF8_HORIZONTAL_ONLY, Table};\nuse sea_orm::EntityTrait;\nuse std::env;\n\nuse crate::{db, entity::repository};\n\npub async fn run() -> Result<()> {\n    let db = db::connect().await?;\n    let repos = repository::Entity::find().all(&db).await?;\n\n    if repos.is_empty() {\n        println!(\"No repositories tracked. Run `gossamer init` in a git repo to get started.\");\n        return Ok(());\n    }\n\n    let cwd = env::current_dir().ok().map(|p| p.to_string_lossy().to_string());\n    let current_repo = cwd.as_deref().and_then(|cwd| {\n        repos.iter().find(|r| cwd.starts_with(r.directory.as_str()))\n    });\n\n    let mut table = Table::new();\n    table.load_preset(UTF8_HORIZONTAL_ONLY);\n    table.set_header([\"\", \"Name\", \"Directory\", \"Remote\"]);\n\n    for repo in &repos {\n        let marker = if current_repo.map_or(false, |r| r.directory == repo.directory) {\n            \"▶\"\n        } else {\n            \"\"\n        };\n        table.add_row([marker, &repo.name, &repo.directory, &repo.remote]);\n    }\n\n    let rendered = table.to_string();\n    println!(\"{}\", colorize(&rendered, current_repo.map(|r| r.directory.as_str())));\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nfn colorize(s: &str, current_dir: Option<&str>) -> String {\n    let Some(dir) = current_dir else { return s.to_string() };\n    // Color the marker and the directory of the current repo.\n    s.replace(\"▶\", \"\\x1b[38;5;82m▶\\x1b[0m\")\n     .replace(dir, &format!(\"\\x1b[38;5;82m{dir}\\x1b[0m\"))\n}\n"
}

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/stephanostsoucas/p/gossamer/src/commands/sessions.rs",
  "old_string": "use anyhow::Result;\nuse chrono::{DateTime, Utc};\nuse comfy_table::{presets::UTF8_HORIZONTAL_ONLY, Table};\nuse sea_orm::{EntityTrait, QueryOrder};\n\nuse crate::{db, entity::session};\n\npub async fn run(all: bool) -> Result<()> {\n    let db = db::connect().await?;\n    let sessions = session::Entity::find()\n        .order_by_desc(session::Column::UpdatedAt)\n        .all(&db)\n        .await?;\n\n    let cutoff = Utc::now() - chrono::Duration::days(3);\n    let sessions: Vec<_> = if all {\n        sessions\n    } else {\n        sessions.into_iter().filter(|s| s.updated_at >= cutoff).collect()\n    };",
  "new_string": "use anyhow::Result;\nuse chrono::{DateTime, Utc};\nuse comfy_table::{presets::UTF8_HORIZONTAL_ONLY, Table};\nuse sea_orm::{EntityTrait, QueryOrder};\nuse std::env;\n\nuse crate::{db, entity::{repository, session}};\n\npub async fn run(all: bool) -> Result<()> {\n    let db = db::connect().await?;\n\n    let repos = repository::Entity::find().all(&db).await?;\n    let cwd = env::current_dir().ok().map(|p| p.to_string_lossy().to_string());\n    let current_repo_dir: Option<String> = cwd.as_deref().and_then(|cwd| {\n        repos.iter()\n            .find(|r| cwd.starts_with(r.directory.as_str()))\n            .map(|r| r.directory.clone())\n    });\n\n    let sessions = session::Entity::find()\n        .order_by_desc(session::Column::UpdatedAt)\n        .all(&db)\n        .await?;\n\n    let cutoff = Utc::now() - chrono::Duration::days(3);\n    let sessions: Vec<_> = if all {\n        sessions\n    } else {\n        sessions.into_iter().filter(|s| s.updated_at >= cutoff).collect()\n    };\n\n    // Sessions in the current repo float to the top; both groups keep updated_at order.\n    let sessions: Vec<_> = if let Some(ref dir) = current_repo_dir {\n        let (local, rest): (Vec<_>, Vec<_>) = sessions\n            .into_iter()\n            .partition(|s| s.cwd.starts_with(dir.as_str()));\n        local.into_iter().chain(rest).collect()\n    } else {\n        sessions\n    };"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/stephanostsoucas/p/gossamer/src/commands/status.rs",
  "content": "use anyhow::Result;\nuse comfy_table::{presets::UTF8_HORIZONTAL_ONLY, Table};\nuse sea_orm::EntityTrait;\nuse std::{collections::HashSet, env};\n\nuse crate::{db, entity::repository};\n\npub async fn run() -> Result<()> {\n    let db = db::connect().await?;\n    let repos = repository::Entity::find().all(&db).await?;\n\n    if repos.is_empty() {\n        println!(\"No repositories tracked. Run `gossamer init` in a git repo to get started.\");\n        return Ok(());\n    }\n\n    let cwd = env::current_dir().ok().map(|p| p.to_string_lossy().to_string());\n    let current_dir = cwd.as_deref().and_then(|cwd| {\n        repos.iter()\n            .find(|r| cwd.starts_with(r.directory.as_str()))\n            .map(|r| r.directory.as_str())\n    });\n\n    let mut table = Table::new();\n    table.load_preset(UTF8_HORIZONTAL_ONLY);\n    table.set_header([\"\", \"Name\", \"Directory\", \"Remote\"]);\n\n    for repo in &repos {\n        let marker = if current_dir == Some(repo.directory.as_str()) { \"▶\" } else { \"\" };\n        table.add_row([marker, &repo.name, &repo.directory, &repo.remote]);\n    }\n\n    println!(\"{}\", colorize(&table.to_string(), current_dir));\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nfn colorize(s: &str, current_dir: Option<&str>) -> String {\n    // Pre-compute local line indices before any ANSI codes are injected.\n    let local_lines: HashSet<usize> = if let Some(dir) = current_dir {\n        s.lines().enumerate()\n            .filter(|(_, line)| line.contains(dir))\n            .map(|(i, _)| i)\n            .collect()\n    } else {\n        HashSet::new()\n    };\n\n    let mut out = s.to_string();\n\n    // Highlight the marker and current directory in pale yellow.\n    out = out.replace(\"▶\", \"\\x1b[38;5;229m▶\\x1b[0m\");\n    if let Some(dir) = current_dir {\n        out = out.replace(dir, &format!(\"\\x1b[38;5;229m{dir}\\x1b[0m\"));\n    }\n\n    // Apply base color per line: white for local rows, light grey for everything else.\n    out.lines().enumerate().map(|(i, line)| {\n        let base: u8 = if local_lines.contains(&i) { 255 } else { 245 };\n        let inner = line.replace(\"\\x1b[0m\", &format!(\"\\x1b[0m\\x1b[38;5;{base}m\"));\n        format!(\"\\x1b[38;5;{base}m{inner}\\x1b[0m\")\n    }).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(\"\\n\")\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/stephanostsoucas/p/gossamer/src/commands/sessions.rs",
  "content": "use anyhow::Result;\nuse chrono::{DateTime, Utc};\nuse comfy_table::{presets::UTF8_HORIZONTAL_ONLY, Table};\nuse sea_orm::{EntityTrait, QueryOrder};\nuse std::{collections::HashSet, env};\n\nuse crate::{db, entity::{repository, session}};\n\npub async fn run(all: bool) -> Result<()> {\n    let db = db::connect().await?;\n\n    let repos = repository::Entity::find().all(&db).await?;\n    let cwd = env::current_dir().ok().map(|p| p.to_string_lossy().to_string());\n    let current_repo_dir: Option<String> = cwd.as_deref().and_then(|cwd| {\n        repos.iter()\n            .find(|r| cwd.starts_with(r.directory.as_str()))\n            .map(|r| r.directory.clone())\n    });\n\n    let sessions = session::Entity::find()\n        .order_by_desc(session::Column::UpdatedAt)\n        .all(&db)\n        .await?;\n\n    let cutoff = Utc::now() - chrono::Duration::days(3);\n    let sessions: Vec<_> = if all {\n        sessions\n    } else {\n        sessions.into_iter().filter(|s| s.updated_at >= cutoff).collect()\n    };\n\n    // Sessions in the current repo float to the top; both groups keep updated_at order.\n    let sessions: Vec<_> = if let Some(ref dir) = current_repo_dir {\n        let (local, rest): (Vec<_>, Vec<_>) = sessions\n            .into_iter()\n            .partition(|s| s.cwd.starts_with(dir.as_str()));\n        local.into_iter().chain(rest).collect()\n    } else {\n        sessions\n    };\n\n    if sessions.is_empty() {\n        println!(\"No sessions found.\");\n        return Ok(());\n    }\n\n    let now = Utc::now();\n    let mut table = Table::new();\n    table.load_preset(UTF8_HORIZONTAL_ONLY);\n    table.set_header([\"\", \"Session ID\", \"Last Prompt\", \"Agent\", \"User\", \"CWD\", \"Created\", \"Updated\"]);\n\n    for s in &sessions {\n        let age = (now - s.updated_at).num_seconds();\n        let dot = if age < 3_600 { \"● \" } else if age < 86_400 { \"● \" } else { \"\" };\n\n        table.add_row([\n            dot,\n            s.session_id.as_str(),\n            &truncate(&s.session_name, 50),\n            s.agent_name.as_str(),\n            s.user.as_str(),\n            s.cwd.as_str(),\n            &relative_time(s.created_at),\n            &relative_time(s.updated_at),\n        ]);\n    }\n\n    println!(\"{}\", colorize(&table.to_string(), &sessions, now, current_repo_dir.as_deref()));\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nfn colorize(s: &str, sessions: &[session::Model], now: DateTime<Utc>, current_repo_dir: Option<&str>) -> String {\n    const AGENTS: &[(&str, u8)] = &[\n        (\"Claude Code\", 214),\n        (\"Copilot\",     99),\n        (\"Cursor\",      33),\n        (\"Gemini\",      75),\n        (\"Aider\",       42),\n        (\"ChatGPT\",     35),\n        (\"Windsurf\",    44),\n        (\"Amazon Q\",    208),\n    ];\n\n    // Pre-compute local line indices before any ANSI codes are injected.\n    let local_lines: HashSet<usize> = if let Some(dir) = current_repo_dir {\n        s.lines().enumerate()\n            .filter(|(_, line)| line.contains(dir))\n            .map(|(i, _)| i)\n            .collect()\n    } else {\n        HashSet::new()\n    };\n\n    let mut out = s.to_string();\n\n    // Current repo CWD in pale yellow.\n    if let Some(dir) = current_repo_dir {\n        out = out.replace(dir, &format!(\"\\x1b[38;5;229m{dir}\\x1b[0m\"));\n    }\n\n    // Agent name colors.\n    for (name, code) in AGENTS {\n        out = out.replace(name, &format!(\"\\x1b[38;5;{code}m{name}\\x1b[0m\"));\n    }\n\n    // Activity dot colors.\n    for session in sessions {\n        let age = (now - session.updated_at).num_seconds();\n        let (dot, code) = if age < 3_600 {\n            (\"● \", 82)\n        } else if age < 86_400 {\n            (\"● \", 214)\n        } else {\n            continue;\n        };\n        out = out.replacen(dot, &format!(\"\\x1b[38;5;{code}m●\\x1b[0m \"), 1);\n    }\n\n    // Apply base color per line: white for local rows, light grey for everything else.\n    out.lines().enumerate().map(|(i, line)| {\n        let base: u8 = if local_lines.contains(&i) { 255 } else { 245 };\n        let inner = line.replace(\"\\x1b[0m\", &format!(\"\\x1b[0m\\x1b[38;5;{base}m\"));\n        format!(\"\\x1b[38;5;{base}m{inner}\\x1b[0m\")\n    }).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(\"\\n\")\n}\n\nfn relative_time(dt: DateTime<Utc>) -> String {\n    let secs = (Utc::now() - dt).num_seconds();\n    match secs {\n        s if s < 60 => \"just now\".to_string(),\n        s if s < 3_600 => format!(\"{} min{} ago\", s / 60, if s / 60 == 1 { \"\" } else { \"s\" }),\n        s if s < 86_400 => format!(\"{} hr{} ago\", s / 3_600, if s / 3_600 == 1 { \"\" } else { \"s\" }),\n        s if s < 604_800 => format!(\"{} day{} ago\", s / 86_400, if s / 86_400 == 1 { \"\" } else { \"s\" }),\n        s if s < 2_592_000 => format!(\"{} wk{} ago\", s / 604_800, if s / 604_800 == 1 { \"\" } else { \"s\" }),\n        s if s < 31_536_000 => format!(\"{} mo ago\", s / 2_592_000),\n        s => format!(\"{} yr{} ago\", s / 31_536_000, if s / 31_536_000 == 1 { \"\" } else { \"s\" }),\n    }\n}\n\nfn truncate(s: &str, max: usize) -> String {\n    if s.chars().count() <= max {\n        s.to_string()\n    } else {\n        format!(\"{}…\", s.chars().take(max - 1).collect::<String>())\n    }\n}\n"
}

> AGENT

The ANSI codes are exactly right:
- Separators and non-local rows: `38;5;245` (light grey)
- Current repo / local session rows: `38;5;255` (white)  
- Marker `▶` and CWD: `38;5;229` (pale yellow)
- After each content color reset, base color is re-applied so the rest of the row stays the right shade
